-- Enable cryptographic functions for UUIDs and random code generation
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- ============================================================================

    WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

    WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN

DO $$ BEGIN

END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. Table: subscription_plans (قوالب الباقات العامة - تُدار بواسطة super_admin)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.subscription_plans (
    code public.plan_tier PRIMARY KEY,
    name_ar TEXT NOT NULL,
    description_ar TEXT,
    default_monthly_price NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (default_monthly_price >= 0),
    max_apartments INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 20 CHECK (max_apartments > 0),
    default_features_config JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{
        "receipt_upload": true,
        "emergency_claims": true,
        "export_reports": true,
        "voting": true,
        "maintenance_tickets": true,
        "documents_archive": true
    }'::jsonb,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.subscription_plans IS 'قوالب الباقات العامة (free, pro, plus) التي يديرها مالك التطبيق (super_admin)';

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. Table: buildings (العمارات والأبراج السكنية)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.buildings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    address TEXT,
    city TEXT DEFAULT 'القاهرة',
    registration_number TEXT, -- مثال: قيد اتحاد الشاغلين رقم 142 لسنة 2023
    building_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, -- كود المبنى الفريد لانضمام السكان
    admin_id UUID, -- يُربط بجدول users في ملف 003
    fund_balance NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00, -- رصيد الصندوق الحالي (الخزينة)
    default_monthly_dues NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (default_monthly_dues >= 0),
    total_apartments INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 16 CHECK (total_apartments > 0),
    
    -- إعدادات الاشتراك والتسعير المرن ومفاتيح المميزات (Feature Flags & Custom Overrides)
    plan_code public.plan_tier NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pro' REFERENCES public.subscription_plans(code),
    subscription_status public.building_subscription_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'trial',
    trial_ends_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + INTERVAL '60 days'),
    subscription_ends_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + INTERVAL '60 days'),
    custom_monthly_price NUMERIC(12, 2) CHECK (custom_monthly_price IS NULL OR custom_monthly_price >= 0),
    max_apartments_override INTEGER CHECK (max_apartments_override IS NULL OR max_apartments_override > 0),
    features_config JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{
        "receipt_upload": true,
        "emergency_claims": true,
        "export_reports": true,
        "voting": true,
        "maintenance_tickets": true,
        "documents_archive": true
    }'::jsonb,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.buildings IS 'بيانات العمارات السكنية، رصيد الخزينة، وإعدادات الباقة المخصصة ومفاتيح المميزات لكل عمارة';

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. Table: apartments (الوحدات السكنية داخل كل عمارة)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.apartments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_number TEXT NOT NULL,
    floor_number TEXT,
    wing_or_direction TEXT, -- مثال: واجهة / المدخل الرئيسي
    area_sqm NUMERIC(8, 2) CHECK (area_sqm IS NULL OR area_sqm > 0),
    ownership_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 6.25 CHECK (ownership_share_percent >= 0 AND ownership_share_percent <= 100),
    monthly_dues_override NUMERIC(12, 2) CHECK (monthly_dues_override IS NULL OR monthly_dues_override >= 0),
    occupant_name TEXT, -- الاسم المبدئي المسجل مسبقاً بواسطة المدير قبل انضمام الساكن
    occupant_phone TEXT, -- رقم الهاتف المسجل مسبقاً للمطابقة التلقائية عند انضمام الساكن
    arrears_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00, -- إجمالي المتأخرات/الالتزامات المسجلة على الشقة
    resident_id UUID, -- يُربط بجدول users في ملف 003
    invite_code TEXT UNIQUE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_building_apartment_number UNIQUE (building_id, apartment_number)
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.apartments IS 'الوحدات السكنية (الشقق) داخل كل عمارة مع الاسم/الهاتف المبدئي (occupant_name, occupant_phone) والمتأخرات المسجلة عليها';

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. Table: users (المستخدمون: admin, resident, super_admin)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.users (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    email TEXT,
    phone TEXT,
    role public.user_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'resident',
    status public.account_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    building_id UUID REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    apartment_number TEXT,
    floor_number TEXT,
    residency_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'owner_resident', -- owner_resident, tenant, non_resident_owner
    ownership_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) DEFAULT 6.25,
    avatar_url TEXT,
    push_token TEXT,
    push_token_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.users IS 'ملفات المستخدمين المرتبطة بـ Supabase Auth مع حماية صارمة لعمود الصلاحية (role) وحفظ الـ push_token للإشعارات الفورية';


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\002_financial_and_operations_tables.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 002_financial_and_operations_tables.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Creates financial and operational tables:
--              emergency_claims, emergency_claim_shares, payments, expenses,
--              app_subscription_invoices, maintenance_tickets, polls, poll_votes,
--              building_documents, and notifications.
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Table: emergency_claims (نظام المطالبات الطارئة وشريط التقدم الحي)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.emergency_claims (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL, -- مثال: تغيير طلمبة المياه
    description TEXT,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'مياه وسباكة', -- مياه وسباكة، مصاعد كهربائية، أمن وحراسة، إلخ
    target_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (target_amount > 0),
    collected_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (collected_amount >= 0),
    distribution_type public.claim_distribution_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'equal', -- equal أو custom
    default_share_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) CHECK (default_share_amount IS NULL OR default_share_amount >= 0),
    due_date DATE,
    status public.claim_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.emergency_claims IS 'المطالبات المالية الطارئة مع تتبع المبلغ المحصل وشريط التقدم وطريقة التوزيع (بالتساوي أو مخصص)';

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. Table: emergency_claim_shares (توزيع أنصبة المطالبة الطارئة على الشقق)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.emergency_claim_shares (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    claim_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.emergency_claims(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    resident_id UUID REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    assigned_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (assigned_amount >= 0),
    paid_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00 CHECK (paid_amount >= 0),
    status public.payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unpaid',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_claim_apartment_share UNIQUE (claim_id, apartment_id)
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.emergency_claim_shares IS 'أنصبة الوحدات السكنية من كل مطالبة طارئة سواء بالتساوي أو بمبالغ مخصصة';

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. Table: payments (مدفوعات السكان: اشتراكات شهرية ومطالبات طارئة)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.payments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    resident_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    payment_category public.payment_category_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monthly_dues',
    emergency_claim_id UUID REFERENCES public.emergency_claims(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    billing_month TEXT NOT NULL, -- بصيغة YYYY-MM مثل '2024-11'
    amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    payment_method public.payment_method_type NOT NULL, -- electronic_transfer أو cash
    cash_recipient public.cash_recipient_type, -- admin (رئيس الاتحاد) أو guard (حارس العمارة) عند الدفع كاش
    receipt_image_url TEXT, -- رابط صورة إيصال التحويل الإلكتروني (InstaPay / تحويل بنكي)
    reference_number TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    status public.payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, -- يُسجل حصرياً عبر auth.uid()
    reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_method_details CHECK (
        (payment_method = 'cash' AND cash_recipient IS NOT NULL)
        OR
        (payment_method = 'electronic_transfer')
    )
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.payments IS 'سجل مدفوعات السكان (تحويل إلكتروني مع إيصال أو كاش للمدير/الحارس) وحالات اعتمادها';

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. Table: app_subscription_invoices (فواتير اشتراك العمارة في التطبيق)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.app_subscription_invoices (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    submitted_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    plan_code public.plan_tier NOT NULL REFERENCES public.subscription_plans(code),
    amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    duration_months INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (duration_months > 0 AND duration_months <= 36),
    receipt_image_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    notes TEXT,
    status public.invoice_review_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    activated_by UUID REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, -- يُسجل حصرياً عبر auth.uid() للـ super_admin
    activated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.app_subscription_invoices IS 'طلبات تفعيل وتجديد اشتراك العمارة المرفوعة من رئيس الاتحاد ليعتمدها الـ super_admin';

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. Table: expenses (لوحة المصروفات والشفافية وسندات الصرف)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.expenses (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL, -- مثال: راتب الحارس، صيانة المصعد الدوري، رسوم اشتراك تطبيق إدارة العقار
    description TEXT,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'صيانة ومرافق',
    amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    expense_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    expense_month TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT to_char(CURRENT_DATE, 'YYYY-MM'),
    invoice_image_url TEXT, -- صورة الفاتورة / الإيصال الورقي للشفافية أمام السكان
    invoice_number TEXT, -- مثال: #INV-4921
    voucher_number TEXT, -- مثال: PC-2024-884
    is_system_generated BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false, -- true عند الإدراج التلقائي لرسوم اشتراك التطبيق
    app_subscription_invoice_id UUID UNIQUE REFERENCES public.app_subscription_invoices(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.expenses IS 'سجل مصروفات العمارة وصور الفواتير المتاح للعرض بشفافية كاملة أمام جميع سكان العمارة';

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. Supporting Tables (البلاغات، التصويت، الوثائق، والتنبيهات من شاشات التصميم)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.maintenance_tickets (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    reported_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عام',
    priority TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'normal', -- low, normal, high, urgent
    image_url TEXT,
    status public.ticket_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.polls (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    session_code TEXT, -- مثال: #24-ORD-08
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    quorum_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 51.00,
    voting_basis TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ownership_share', -- ownership_share أو one_vote_per_unit
    ends_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    status public.poll_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.poll_votes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    poll_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.polls(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    voter_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    choice TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (choice IN ('approve', 'reject', 'abstain')),
    weight_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 6.25,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_poll_apartment_vote UNIQUE (poll_id, apartment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.building_documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    uploaded_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'اللائحة والعقود',
    file_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size_bytes BIGINT,
    is_official BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.notifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    recipient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    body TEXT NOT NULL,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'general', -- financial, voting, maintenance, general
    is_read BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ============================================================================
-- 7. Table: apartment_obligations (الالتزامات والمديونيات المسجلة على الشقة)
--    يدعم تسجيل المتأخرات قبل انضمام الساكن، ونقلها بين الشقق عبر
--    دالة transfer_apartment_obligations دون الحاجة لحذفها وإعادة كتابتها.
-- ============================================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.apartment_obligations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    category public.payment_category_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monthly_dues',
    billing_month TEXT NOT NULL,
    amount NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
    status public.payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unpaid',
    notes TEXT,
    transferred_from_apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    transferred_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

COMMENT ON TABLE public.apartment_obligations IS 'سجل الالتزامات والمتأخرات المقيدة على كل شقة مع دعم النقل الآمن بين الشقق (transfer_apartment_obligations)';



-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\003_relationships_indexes_and_triggers.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 003_relationships_indexes_and_triggers.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Adds deferred foreign keys, performance indexes, security helper
--              functions relying on auth.uid(), and protective triggers
--              (Role Protection & Read-Only Subscription Enforcement).
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Deferred Cross-Table Foreign Key Constraints
-- ============================================================================

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE public.buildings
        ADD CONSTRAINT fk_buildings_admin_user
        FOREIGN KEY (admin_id) REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
EXCEPTION
    WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE public.apartments
        ADD CONSTRAINT fk_apartments_resident_user
        FOREIGN KEY (resident_id) REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
EXCEPTION
    WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. Performance Indexes
-- ============================================================================

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_buildings_building_code ON public.buildings(building_code);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_buildings_admin_id ON public.buildings(admin_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_buildings_subscription_status ON public.buildings(subscription_status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_apartments_building_id ON public.apartments(building_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_apartments_resident_id ON public.apartments(resident_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_building_id ON public.users(building_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_role_status ON public.users(role, status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_building_status ON public.payments(building_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_resident_id ON public.payments(resident_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_billing_month ON public.payments(building_id, billing_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_emergency_claim_id ON public.payments(emergency_claim_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_emergency_claims_building_status ON public.emergency_claims(building_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_emergency_claim_shares_claim_id ON public.emergency_claim_shares(claim_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_emergency_claim_shares_resident_id ON public.emergency_claim_shares(resident_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expenses_building_month ON public.expenses(building_id, expense_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expenses_building_date ON public.expenses(building_id, expense_date DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_app_sub_invoices_building_status ON public.app_subscription_invoices(building_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_notifications_recipient_unread ON public.notifications(recipient_id, is_read);

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. Security Helper Functions (Strictly using auth.uid())
-- ============================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_role()
RETURNS public.user_role
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
    SELECT role FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_building_id()
RETURNS UUID
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
    SELECT building_id FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_status()
RETURNS public.account_status
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
    SELECT status FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
$$;

-- Checks whether a building's subscription or 60-day trial is currently active (Not Read-Only)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_building_writable(p_building_id UUID)
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_building RECORD;
BEGIN
    SELECT subscription_status, trial_ends_at, subscription_ends_at
    INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = p_building_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN false;
    END IF;

    IF v_building.subscription_status IN ('expired', 'read_only') THEN
        RETURN false;
    END IF;

    IF v_building.subscription_status = 'trial' AND v_building.trial_ends_at < now() THEN
        RETURN false;
    END IF;

    IF v_building.subscription_status = 'active' AND v_building.subscription_ends_at < now() THEN
        RETURN false;
    END IF;

    RETURN true;
END;
$$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. Trigger: Auto-update updated_at timestamp
-- ============================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DO $$
DECLARE
    t TEXT;
BEGIN
    FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY[
        'subscription_plans',
        'buildings',
        'apartments',
        'users',
        'emergency_claims',
        'emergency_claim_shares',
        'payments',
        'app_subscription_invoices',
        'expenses',
        'maintenance_tickets',
        'polls'
    ] LOOP
        EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_set_updated_at ON public.%I;', t);
        EXECUTE format(
            'CREATE TRIGGER trg_set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.%I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();',
            t
        );
    END LOOP;
END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. Security Mandate #1 Trigger: Strict Role & Sensitive Column Protection
--    Prevents ANY user from modifying their own role to super_admin/admin or
--    tampering with status/building_id outside authorized RPC contexts.
-- ============================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_user_sensitive_columns()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_caller_building UUID;
    v_bypass TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Allow trusted internal SECURITY DEFINER RPC functions that explicitly set a local session flag
    v_bypass := current_setting('app.rpc_internal_bypass', true);
    IF v_bypass = 'true' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Allow service_role (migrations / backend admin) when auth.uid() is null
    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT role, building_id INTO v_caller_role, v_caller_building
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = auth.uid();

    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        -- On direct client INSERT, force role = 'resident' and status = 'pending'
        -- (Becoming 'admin' happens exclusively through the secure RPC create_building_for_admin)
        IF COALESCE(v_caller_role, 'resident'::public.user_role) <> 'super_admin' THEN
            IF NEW.role <> 'resident' THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Cannot self-assign privileged role (%).', NEW.role;
            END IF;
            NEW.status := 'pending';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
        -- Only super_admin can ever change a user's role directly
        IF NEW.role IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.role AND v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Modifying user role is strictly prohibited.';
        END IF;

        -- Prevent non-super_admin and non-building-admin from changing status or building_id
        IF v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
            IF NEW.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.building_id AND OLD.building_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Cannot change assigned building_id.';
            END IF;

            IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status THEN
                -- Only the active admin of the same building can change a resident's status
                IF NOT (v_caller_role = 'admin' AND v_caller_building = OLD.building_id AND OLD.role = 'resident') THEN
                    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Unauthorized attempt to change account status.';
                END IF;
            END IF;
        END IF;

        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_user_sensitive_columns ON public.users;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_protect_user_sensitive_columns
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON public.users
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION public.protect_user_sensitive_columns();

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. Trigger: Protect Building Financial & Subscription Fields from Direct Edit
-- ============================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_building_sensitive_columns()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_bypass TEXT;
BEGIN
    v_bypass := current_setting('app.rpc_internal_bypass', true);
    IF v_bypass = 'true' OR auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT role INTO v_caller_role FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();

    -- Only internal RPCs can modify fund_balance
    IF NEW.fund_balance IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.fund_balance THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: fund_balance can only be modified via verified financial RPC functions.';
    END IF;

    -- Only super_admin can modify subscription pricing, limits, dates, or feature flags
    IF v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
        IF NEW.plan_code IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.plan_code
           OR NEW.subscription_status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.subscription_status
           OR NEW.trial_ends_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.trial_ends_at
           OR NEW.subscription_ends_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.subscription_ends_at
           OR NEW.custom_monthly_price IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.custom_monthly_price
           OR NEW.max_apartments_override IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.max_apartments_override
           OR NEW.features_config IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.features_config
           OR NEW.building_code IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.building_code
        THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Subscription settings and feature flags are restricted to Super Admin.';
        END IF;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_building_sensitive_columns ON public.buildings;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_protect_building_sensitive_columns
    BEFORE UPDATE ON public.buildings
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION public.protect_building_sensitive_columns();

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. RLS Policies: subscription_plans (قوالب الباقات العامة)
-- ============================================================================

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "plans_select_authenticated" ON public.subscription_plans;
CREATE POLICY "plans_select_authenticated"
    ON public.subscription_plans
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (is_active = true OR public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin');

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "plans_manage_super_admin" ON public.subscription_plans;
CREATE POLICY "plans_manage_super_admin"
    ON public.subscription_plans
    FOR ALL
    TO authenticated
    USING (public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin')
    WITH CHECK (public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin');

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. RLS Policies: users (حماية صارمة لعمود role وبيانات المستخدمين)
-- ============================================================================

-- قراءة بيانات المستخدمين (للسكان والمدير لمتابعة طلبات الانضمام والأعضاء)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_select_policy" ON public.users;
CREATE POLICY "users_select_policy"
    ON public.users
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated, anon
    USING (true);

-- إنشاء المستخدم لحسابه الأولي فقط بصلاحية resident وحالة pending
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_insert_self_only" ON public.users;
CREATE POLICY "users_insert_self_only"
    ON public.users
    FOR INSERT
    TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (
        id = auth.uid()
        AND role = 'resident'
        AND status = 'pending'
    );

-- تحديث المستخدم لبياناته الأساسية فقط دون المساس بـ role (محمي أيضاً بـ Trigger)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_update_self_safe" ON public.users;
CREATE POLICY "users_update_self_safe"
    ON public.users
    FOR UPDATE
    TO authenticated
    USING (id = auth.uid())
    WITH CHECK (
        id = auth.uid()
        AND role = public.get_my_role()
    );

-- السماح لمدير العمارة بتحديث حالة السكان في عمارته، وللـ super_admin بإدارة الجميع
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_update_admin_and_super_admin" ON public.users;
CREATE POLICY "users_update_admin_and_super_admin"
    ON public.users
    FOR UPDATE
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND role = 'resident'
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND role = 'resident'
            AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
        )
    );

-- 4. RLS Policies: buildings (العمارات وإعدادات التسعير والمميزات)

CREATE POLICY "buildings_select_policy"
    ON public.buildings

    TO authenticated, anon
    USING (true);

            admin_id = auth.uid()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.is_building_writable(id)
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            admin_id = auth.uid()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.is_building_writable(id)
        )
    );

-- 11. Supabase Storage Buckets & Policies (صور الإيصالات والفواتير والوثائق)
-- ============================================================================

INSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
VALUES
    ('payment-receipts', 'payment-receipts', false, 5242880, ARRAY['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'application/pdf']),
    ('expense-invoices', 'expense-invoices', false, 5242880, ARRAY['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'application/pdf']),
    ('subscription-receipts', 'subscription-receipts', false, 5242880, ARRAY['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp', 'application/pdf']),
    ('building-documents', 'building-documents', false, 10485760, ARRAY['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'application/pdf'])
ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;

-- Storage Select Policy: Authenticated users can view files belonging to their building folder
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "storage_building_members_select" ON storage.objects;
CREATE POLICY "storage_building_members_select"
    ON storage.objects
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        bucket_id IN ('payment-receipts', 'expense-invoices', 'subscription-receipts', 'building-documents')
        AND (
            public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
            OR (storage.foldername(name))[1] = public.get_my_building_id()::text
        )
    );

-- Storage Insert Policy: Residents upload payment receipts; Admins upload expense/subscription/building docs
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "storage_authenticated_upload" ON storage.objects;
CREATE POLICY "storage_authenticated_upload"
    ON storage.objects
    FOR INSERT
    TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (
        (storage.foldername(name))[1] = public.get_my_building_id()::text
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        AND (
            (bucket_id = 'payment-receipts')
            OR (
                bucket_id IN ('expense-invoices', 'subscription-receipts', 'building-documents')
                AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            )
        )
    );

-- ============================================================================
-- 12. Enable Supabase Realtime Publication for Live Cross-Device Sync
-- ============================================================================

ALTER TABLE public.notifications REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.users REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.payments REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.apartments REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.expenses REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.emergency_claims REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;

DO $$
BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE 
        public.notifications,
        public.users,
        public.payments,
        public.apartments,
        public.expenses,
        public.emergency_claims;
EXCEPTION
    WHEN duplicate_object THEN
        NULL;
    WHEN undefined_object THEN
        CREATE PUBLICATION supabase_realtime FOR TABLE 
            public.notifications,
            public.users,
            public.payments,
            public.apartments,
            public.expenses,
            public.emergency_claims;
END;
$$;
