ALTER TABLE public.polls ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.poll_votes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- building_issues Policies
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "issues_select_policy" ON public.building_issues;
CREATE POLICY "issues_select_policy"
    ON public.building_issues
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND (
                -- Admin sees everything
                public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
                -- Residents see public issues or their own private issues
                OR is_private = false
                OR created_by = auth.uid()
            )
        )
    );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "issues_insert_policy" ON public.building_issues;
CREATE POLICY "issues_insert_policy"
    ON public.building_issues
    FOR INSERT
    TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND created_by = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
    );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "issues_update_policy" ON public.building_issues;
CREATE POLICY "issues_update_policy"
    ON public.building_issues
    FOR UPDATE
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND (
                public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
                OR (created_by = auth.uid() AND status = 'open')
            )
        )
    );

-- issue_upvotes Policies
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "upvotes_select_policy" ON public.issue_upvotes;
CREATE POLICY "upvotes_select_policy"
    ON public.issue_upvotes
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "upvotes_manage_policy" ON public.issue_upvotes;
CREATE POLICY "upvotes_manage_policy"
    ON public.issue_upvotes
    FOR ALL
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND user_id = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND user_id = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
    );

-- polls Policies
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "polls_select_policy" ON public.polls;
CREATE POLICY "polls_select_policy"
    ON public.polls
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "polls_manage_admin" ON public.polls;
CREATE POLICY "polls_manage_admin"
    ON public.polls
    FOR ALL
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        )
    );

-- poll_votes Policies
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "votes_select_policy" ON public.poll_votes;
CREATE POLICY "votes_select_policy"
    ON public.poll_votes
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "votes_insert_manage_policy" ON public.poll_votes;
CREATE POLICY "votes_insert_manage_policy"
    ON public.poll_votes
    FOR ALL
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND voter_id = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND voter_id = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
    );

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. Realtime Publication
-- ============================================================================

ALTER TABLE public.building_issues REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.issue_upvotes REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.polls REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.poll_votes REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;

DO $$
BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE 
        public.building_issues,
        public.issue_upvotes,
        public.polls,
        public.poll_votes;
EXCEPTION
    WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL;
    WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL;
END;
$$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 7. Secure RPC Functions
-- ============================================================================

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- RPC 1: create_building_issue
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_building_issue(
    p_building_id UUID,
    p_title TEXT,
    p_description TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_category TEXT DEFAULT 'المصعد',
    p_severity TEXT DEFAULT 'medium',
    p_image_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_is_private BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    p_preferred_time TEXT DEFAULT 'any',
    p_whatsapp_notifications BOOLEAN DEFAULT true
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_issue_id UUID;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, apartment_id, apartment_number, full_name, role, status
    INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User profile is not active.';
    END IF;

    IF v_user.building_id <> p_building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User does not belong to this building.';
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO public.building_issues (
        building_id,
        created_by,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        reporter_name,
        title,
        description,
        category,
        severity,
        image_url,
        is_private,
        preferred_time,
        whatsapp_notifications,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        p_building_id,
        v_caller_id,
        v_user.apartment_id,
        COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '1'),
        COALESCE(v_user.full_name, 'ساكن'),
        trim(p_title),
        trim(p_description),
        COALESCE(p_category, 'المصعد'),
        COALESCE(p_severity, 'medium'),
        p_image_url,
        COALESCE(p_is_private, false),
        COALESCE(p_preferred_time, 'any'),
        COALESCE(p_whatsapp_notifications, true),
        'open'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_issue_id;

    -- Send notification to building admin
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    )
    SELECT
        p_building_id,
        'admin',
        'بلاغ صيانة جديد (شقة ' || COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '-') || ') 🛠️',
        'تم تقديم بلاغ جديد بعنوان: "' || trim(p_title) || '" في قسم (' || COALESCE(p_category, 'عام') || ').',
        'maintenance',
        jsonb_build_object(
            'issue_id', v_issue_id,
            'apartment_number', v_user.apartment_number,
            'severity', p_severity
        );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'issue_id', v_issue_id,
        'title', trim(p_title)
    );
END;
$$;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- RPC 2: toggle_issue_upvote (صوت تأييد واحد لكل شقة سكنية)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.toggle_issue_upvote(
    p_issue_id UUID
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_issue RECORD;
    v_existing_id UUID;
    v_new_count INTEGER;
    v_apt_id UUID;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, apartment_id, apartment_number, role, status
    INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User profile is not active.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_issue
    FROM public.building_issues
    WHERE id = p_issue_id;

    IF v_issue IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Issue not found.';
    END IF;

    IF v_issue.building_id <> v_user.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Issue belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- Fallback apartment_id if null
    v_apt_id := COALESCE(v_user.apartment_id, v_caller_id);

    -- Check if this apartment already upvoted
    SELECT id INTO v_existing_id
    FROM public.issue_upvotes
    WHERE issue_id = p_issue_id
      AND (apartment_id = v_user.apartment_id OR (v_user.apartment_id IS NULL AND user_id = v_caller_id));

    IF v_existing_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        -- Remove upvote
        DELETE FROM public.issue_upvotes WHERE id = v_existing_id;
        UPDATE public.building_issues
        SET upvotes_count = GREATEST(0, upvotes_count - 1),
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = p_issue_id
        RETURNING upvotes_count INTO v_new_count;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'action', 'removed',
            'upvoted', false,
            'upvotes_count', v_new_count
        );
    ELSE
        -- Add upvote
        INSERT INTO public.issue_upvotes (
            issue_id,
            building_id,
            apartment_id,
            apartment_number,
            user_id
        ) VALUES (
            p_issue_id,
            v_user.building_id,
            v_user.apartment_id,
            COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '1'),
            v_caller_id
        );

        UPDATE public.building_issues
        SET upvotes_count = upvotes_count + 1,
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = p_issue_id
        RETURNING upvotes_count INTO v_new_count;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'action', 'added',
            'upvoted', true,
            'upvotes_count', v_new_count
        );
    END IF;
END;
$$;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- RPC 3: update_issue_status (المدير يغير الحالة: open, in_progress, resolved)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.update_issue_status(
    p_issue_id UUID,
    p_status TEXT
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_issue RECORD;
    v_status_ar TEXT;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, role, status INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.role <> 'admin' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Only building admin can update issue status.';
    END IF;

    IF p_status NOT IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'resolved') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid status. Must be open, in_progress, or resolved.';
    END IF;

    UPDATE public.building_issues
    SET status = p_status,
        resolved_at = CASE WHEN p_status = 'resolved' THEN now() ELSE NULL END,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_issue_id AND building_id = v_user.building_id
    RETURNING * INTO v_issue;

    IF v_issue IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Issue not found in your building.';
    END IF;

    v_status_ar := CASE p_status
        WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 'جاري التنفيذ والمتابعة ⏳'
        WHEN 'resolved' THEN 'تم الإصلاح والحل بنجاح ✅'
        ELSE 'مفتوح للمتابعة 🟢'
    END;

    -- Send notification to the reporter resident
    IF v_issue.created_by IS NOT NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO public.notifications (
            building_id,
            recipient_id,
            title,
            body,
            category,
            metadata
        ) VALUES (
            v_issue.building_id,
            v_issue.created_by,
            'تحديث بخصوص بلاغك 🛠️',
            'تم تحديث حالة بلاغك ("' || v_issue.title || '") إلى: ' || v_status_ar,
            'maintenance',
            jsonb_build_object('issue_id', v_issue.id, 'status', p_status)
        );
    END IF;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'issue_id', v_issue.id,
        'status', p_status,
        'status_ar', v_status_ar
    );
END;
$$;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- RPC 4: create_building_poll (المدير يطرح قراراً وتصويتاً للجمعية العمومية)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_building_poll(
    p_building_id UUID,
    p_title TEXT,
    p_description TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_category TEXT DEFAULT 'عام',
    p_estimated_cost NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    p_share_per_unit NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    p_quorum_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) DEFAULT 51.00,
    p_duration_days INTEGER DEFAULT 7,
    p_options JSONB DEFAULT NULL,
    p_document_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_is_urgent BOOLEAN DEFAULT false
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_poll_id UUID;
    v_session_code TEXT;
    v_options JSONB;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, role, status INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.role <> 'admin' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Only building admin can create polls.';
    END IF;

    v_session_code := '#' || to_char(CURRENT_DATE, 'YY') || '-DEC-' || upper(substr(replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 1, 4));

    IF p_options IS NULL OR jsonb_array_length(p_options) = 0 THEN
        v_options := '[{"id":"approve","title":"موافق على القرار"},{"id":"reject","title":"رافض أو متحفظ"},{"id":"abstain","title":"ممتنع عن التصويت"}]'::jsonb;
    ELSE
        v_options := p_options;
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO public.polls (
        building_id,
        created_by,
        session_code,
        title,
        description,
        category,
        estimated_cost,
        share_per_unit,
        quorum_percent,
        ends_at,
        options,
        document_url,
        is_urgent,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        p_building_id,
        v_caller_id,
        v_session_code,
        trim(p_title),
        trim(p_description),
        COALESCE(p_category, 'عام'),
        COALESCE(p_estimated_cost, 0.00),
        COALESCE(p_share_per_unit, 0.00),
        COALESCE(p_quorum_percent, 51.00),
        now() + (COALESCE(p_duration_days, 7) || ' days')::interval,
        v_options,
        p_document_url,
        COALESCE(p_is_urgent, false),
        'active'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_poll_id;

    -- Broadcast notification to all active residents in building
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    )
    VALUES (
        p_building_id,
        'resident',
        'تصويت وقرار جديد بانتظار مشاركتك 🗳️',
        'طرحت إدارة العمارة قراراً جديداً للتصويت: "' || trim(p_title) || '". صوتك يمثل شقتك ويوثق قانونياً.',
        'voting',
        jsonb_build_object('poll_id', v_poll_id, 'session_code', v_session_code)
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'poll_id', v_poll_id,
        'session_code', v_session_code
    );
END;
$$;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- RPC 5: cast_poll_vote (تسجيل صوت موثق بصوت واحد لكل شقة)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cast_poll_vote(
    p_poll_id UUID,
    p_choice TEXT
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_poll RECORD;
    v_total_apts INTEGER;
    v_weight NUMERIC(5, 2);
    v_votes_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, apartment_id, apartment_number, full_name, role, status
    INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User profile is not active.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_poll
    FROM public.polls
    WHERE id = p_poll_id;

    IF v_poll IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Poll not found.';
    END IF;

    IF v_poll.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'This poll is no longer active.';
    END IF;

    IF v_poll.ends_at < now() THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Voting deadline has ended.';
    END IF;

    IF v_poll.building_id <> v_user.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Poll belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- Calculate unit weight based on total building apartments
    SELECT total_apartments INTO v_total_apts
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_user.building_id;

    v_weight := ROUND(100.0 / GREATEST(COALESCE(v_total_apts, 16), 1), 2);

    -- Upsert vote strictly 1 vote per apartment
    INSERT INTO public.poll_votes (
        poll_id,
        building_id,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        voter_id,
        voter_name,
        choice,
        weight_percent
    ) VALUES (
        p_poll_id,
        v_user.building_id,
        COALESCE(v_user.apartment_id, v_caller_id),
        COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '1'),
        v_caller_id,
        COALESCE(v_user.full_name, 'ساكن'),
        trim(p_choice),
        v_weight
    )
    ON CONFLICT (poll_id, apartment_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        choice = EXCLUDED.choice,
        voter_id = EXCLUDED.voter_id,
        voter_name = EXCLUDED.voter_name,
        created_at = now();

    -- Update total votes count on poll
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_votes_count
    FROM public.poll_votes
    WHERE poll_id = p_poll_id;

    UPDATE public.polls
    SET votes_count = v_votes_count,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_poll_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'poll_id', p_poll_id,
        'choice', trim(p_choice),
        'apartment_number', v_user.apartment_number,
        'votes_count', v_votes_count
    );
END;
$$;


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\008_security_patch_impersonation_rpcs.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 008_security_patch_impersonation_rpcs.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Step 2 Security Patch:
--              1. Adds contributors_count to emergency_claims.
--              2. Rewrites the 5 impersonation RPC functions:
--                 - review_resident_join_request
--                 - approve_resident_payment
--                 - reject_resident_payment
--                 - submit_resident_payment
--                 - create_emergency_claim
--                 - create_building_poll
--              3. Strictly enforces caller identity via auth.uid() ONLY.
--                 Eliminates p_admin_id / p_resident_id / fallback to any admin or building.
--              4. Enforces subscription validity via is_building_writable().
--              5. Adds FOR UPDATE concurrency locks and idempotency checks.
--              6. Enforces REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon and GRANT TO authenticated.
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Schema Patch: Ensure contributors_count column exists on emergency_claims
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.emergency_claims 
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS contributors_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. RPC: review_resident_join_request
--    Only active building admin can approve/reject resident join requests.
--    No identity parameters or fallbacks permitted.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(
    p_resident_user_id UUID,
    p_approve BOOLEAN,
    p_override_apartment_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_override_apartment_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_resident RECORD;
    v_target_apt RECORD;
    v_final_apt_id UUID;
    v_final_apt_number TEXT;
    v_final_floor TEXT;
    v_new_status public.account_status;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Strict Identity Check via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can review join requests.';
    END IF;

    IF v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Admin is not assigned to an active building.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Check building writability (subscription status)
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_admin.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Lock target resident record FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_resident
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = p_resident_user_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Resident request not found.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Verify resident belongs to the SAME building
    IF v_resident.building_id IS NULL OR v_resident.building_id <> v_admin.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Resident request belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- 5. Idempotency Check: must be 'pending'
    IF v_resident.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'resident_id', p_resident_user_id,
            'new_status', v_resident.status,
            'message', 'Already processed'
        );
    END IF;

    v_final_apt_id := v_resident.apartment_id;
    v_final_apt_number := v_resident.apartment_number;
    v_final_floor := v_resident.floor_number;

    -- 6. Unit Override Handling: must be within the same building
    IF p_approve AND (p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL OR (p_override_apartment_number IS NOT NULL AND trim(p_override_apartment_number) <> '')) THEN
        SELECT * INTO v_target_apt
        FROM public.apartments
        WHERE building_id = v_admin.building_id
          AND (
              (p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL AND id = p_override_apartment_id)
              OR (p_override_apartment_id IS NULL AND trim(apartment_number) = trim(p_override_apartment_number))
          )
        FOR UPDATE;

        IF FOUND THEN
            v_final_apt_id := v_target_apt.id;
            v_final_apt_number := v_target_apt.apartment_number;
            v_final_floor := COALESCE(v_target_apt.floor_number, v_resident.floor_number);
        ELSE
            IF p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Specified override apartment does not exist in this building.';
            END IF;
            v_final_apt_number := trim(p_override_apartment_number);
        END IF;
    END IF;

    v_new_status := CASE WHEN p_approve THEN 'active'::public.account_status ELSE 'rejected'::public.account_status END;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 7. Update resident status and assigned unit
    UPDATE public.users
    SET status = v_new_status,
        apartment_id = v_final_apt_id,
        apartment_number = v_final_apt_number,
        floor_number = v_final_floor,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_resident_user_id;

    -- 8. If approved and apartment unit is linked, bind resident to unit
    IF p_approve AND v_final_apt_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE public.apartments
        SET resident_id = p_resident_user_id,
            occupant_name = COALESCE(occupant_name, v_resident.full_name),
            occupant_phone = COALESCE(occupant_phone, v_resident.phone),
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = v_final_apt_id;
    END IF;

    -- 9. Send notification to resident
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category
    ) VALUES (
        v_admin.building_id,
        p_resident_user_id,
        'resident',
        CASE WHEN p_approve THEN 'تم تفعيل حسابك بنجاح 🎉' ELSE 'تم رفض طلب الانضمام' END,
        CASE WHEN p_approve
            THEN format('تمت موافقة رئيس اتحاد الملاك على انضمامك وتسكينك على شقة رقم (%s). يمكنك الآن استخدام كافة خدمات التطبيق.', COALESCE(v_final_apt_number, '-'))
            ELSE 'نعتذر، لم تتم الموافقة على طلب انضمامك. يرجى التواصل مع رئيس اتحاد الملاك.'
        END,
        'join_request'
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'resident_id', p_resident_user_id,
        'new_status', v_new_status,
        'apartment_id', v_final_apt_id,
        'apartment_number', v_final_apt_number
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. RPC: approve_resident_payment
--    Only active building admin of the payment's building can approve payments.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.approve_resident_payment(uuid, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.approve_resident_payment(uuid);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approve_resident_payment(
    p_payment_id UUID
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_payment RECORD;
    v_new_balance NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_claim RECORD;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can approve payments.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Lock the target payment row FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_payment
    FROM public.payments
    WHERE id = p_payment_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'طلب السداد غير موجود.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Verify payment belongs to admin's building
    IF v_payment.building_id <> v_admin.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Payment belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_payment.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    -- 5. Idempotency Check
    IF v_payment.status = 'paid' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'payment_id', p_payment_id,
            'status', 'paid',
            'message', 'Already approved'
        );
    END IF;

    IF v_payment.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'منع التكرار المزدوج: حالة الدفعة الحالية (%).', v_payment.status;
    END IF;

    -- 6. Emergency claim validation if applicable
    IF v_payment.payment_category = 'emergency_claim' AND v_payment.emergency_claim_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT * INTO v_claim
        FROM public.emergency_claims
        WHERE id = v_payment.emergency_claim_id;

        IF NOT FOUND OR v_claim.building_id <> v_payment.building_id THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'المطالبة الطارئة المرتبطة بهذا السداد لا تخص هذه العمارة.';
        END IF;

        IF v_claim.status <> 'active' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'المطالبة الطارئة المرتبطة لم تعد نشطة (حالتها: %).', v_claim.status;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 7. Mark payment as 'paid'
    UPDATE public.payments
    SET status = 'paid',
        reviewed_by = v_caller_id,
        reviewed_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_payment_id;

    -- 8. Increment building fund_balance
    UPDATE public.buildings
    SET fund_balance = fund_balance + v_payment.amount
    WHERE id = v_payment.building_id
    RETURNING fund_balance INTO v_new_balance;

    -- 9. Update emergency claim totals & shares if applicable
    IF v_payment.payment_category = 'emergency_claim' AND v_payment.emergency_claim_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE public.emergency_claims
        SET collected_amount = collected_amount + v_payment.amount,
            contributors_count = COALESCE(contributors_count, 0) + 1,
            status = CASE
                WHEN (collected_amount + v_payment.amount) >= target_amount THEN 'completed'::public.claim_status
                ELSE status
            END
        WHERE id = v_payment.emergency_claim_id;

        IF v_payment.apartment_id IS NOT NULL THEN
            UPDATE public.emergency_claim_shares
            SET paid_amount = paid_amount + v_payment.amount,
                status = CASE
                    WHEN (paid_amount + v_payment.amount) >= assigned_amount THEN 'paid'::public.payment_status
                    ELSE 'pending'::public.payment_status
                END
            WHERE claim_id = v_payment.emergency_claim_id
              AND apartment_id = v_payment.apartment_id;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- 10. Notify resident
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    ) VALUES (
        v_payment.building_id,
        v_payment.resident_id,
        'resident',
        'تم اعتماد سدادك بنجاح 🟢',
        format('تم اعتماد سدادك بمبلغ %s ج.م عن (%s) وإضافته لخزينة العمارة.', v_payment.amount, COALESCE(v_payment.billing_month, 'مطالبة صيانة طارئة')),
        'financial',
        jsonb_build_object(
            'apartment_number', v_payment.apartment_number,
            'amount', v_payment.amount,
            'payment_id', p_payment_id,
            'category', v_payment.payment_category
        )
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'payment_id', p_payment_id,
        'status', 'paid',
        'approved_by', v_caller_id,
        'added_amount', v_payment.amount,
        'new_fund_balance', v_new_balance
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.approve_resident_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.approve_resident_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. RPC: reject_resident_payment
--    Only active building admin of the payment's building can reject payments.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.reject_resident_payment(uuid, text, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.reject_resident_payment(uuid, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reject_resident_payment(
    p_payment_id UUID,
    p_rejection_reason TEXT DEFAULT 'بيانات الإيصال غير واضحة أو لم يصل التحويل'
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_payment RECORD;
    v_reason TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can reject payments.';
    END IF;

    v_reason := trim(COALESCE(p_rejection_reason, ''));
    IF v_reason = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Rejection reason is required.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Lock the target payment row FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_payment
    FROM public.payments
    WHERE id = p_payment_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'طلب السداد غير موجود.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Verify payment belongs to admin's building
    IF v_payment.building_id <> v_admin.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Payment belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_payment.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    -- 5. Idempotency Check
    IF v_payment.status = 'rejected' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'payment_id', p_payment_id,
            'status', 'rejected',
            'message', 'Already rejected'
        );
    END IF;

    IF v_payment.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'منع التكرار المزدوج: حالة الدفعة الحالية (%).', v_payment.status;
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 6. Mark payment as 'rejected'
    UPDATE public.payments
    SET status = 'rejected',
        reviewed_by = v_caller_id,
        reviewed_at = now(),
        rejection_reason = v_reason
    WHERE id = p_payment_id;

    -- 7. Reset emergency claim share status to 'unpaid' if applicable
    IF v_payment.payment_category = 'emergency_claim' AND v_payment.emergency_claim_id IS NOT NULL AND v_payment.apartment_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE public.emergency_claim_shares
        SET status = 'unpaid'
        WHERE claim_id = v_payment.emergency_claim_id
          AND apartment_id = v_payment.apartment_id;
    END IF;

    -- 8. Notify resident with clear reason
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    ) VALUES (
        v_payment.building_id,
        v_payment.resident_id,
        'resident',
        format('تعذر اعتماد إيصال السداد (شقة %s) ⚠️', COALESCE(v_payment.apartment_number, '-')),
        format('تم رفض طلب السداد بمبلغ %s ج.م. سبب الرفض: "%s". يرجى إعادة المحاولة أو مراجعة رئيس الاتحاد.',
            v_payment.amount, v_reason),
        'financial',
        jsonb_build_object(
            'apartment_number', v_payment.apartment_number,
            'amount', v_payment.amount,
            'payment_id', p_payment_id,
            'rejection_reason', v_reason,
            'category', v_payment.payment_category
        )
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'payment_id', p_payment_id,
        'status', 'rejected',
        'reviewed_by', v_caller_id,
        'rejection_reason', v_reason
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reject_resident_payment(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reject_resident_payment(uuid, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. RPC: submit_resident_payment
--    Only active resident can submit payments.
--    Building, unit, and resident details are strictly retrieved from DB.
--    Regex validation on billing_month and storage bucket check on receipts.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.submit_resident_payment(numeric, public.payment_method_type, text, public.cash_recipient_type, text, public.payment_category_type, uuid, text, text, uuid, text, text, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.submit_resident_payment(numeric, public.payment_method_type, text, public.cash_recipient_type, text, public.payment_category_type, uuid, text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.submit_resident_payment(
    p_amount NUMERIC(12, 2),
    p_payment_method public.payment_method_type,
    p_billing_month TEXT,
    p_cash_recipient public.cash_recipient_type DEFAULT NULL,
    p_receipt_image_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_payment_category public.payment_category_type DEFAULT 'monthly_dues',
    p_emergency_claim_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_reference_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_resident RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_claim RECORD;
    v_payment_id UUID;
    v_apt_id UUID;
    v_apt_number TEXT;
    v_res_name TEXT;
    v_month TEXT;
    v_receipt TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify caller strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Fetch resident profile from users table
    SELECT * INTO v_resident
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_resident.id IS NULL OR v_resident.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: User account is not active or does not exist.';
    END IF;

    IF v_resident.role <> 'resident' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only residents can submit payments via this function.';
    END IF;

    IF v_resident.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Resident is not associated with an active building.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_resident.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_resident.building_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Building not found.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Amount validation
    IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'يجب أن يكون مبلغ السداد أكبر من الصفر.';
    END IF;

    -- 5. Format & validate billing_month (YYYY-MM)
    v_month := trim(COALESCE(p_billing_month, ''));
    IF NOT (v_month ~ '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'صيغة الشهر غير صالحة. يجب أن تكون بصيغة YYYY-MM (مثال: 2026-05).';
    END IF;

    -- 6. Resolve unit number and apartment_id from DB
    v_apt_id := v_resident.apartment_id;
    v_apt_number := v_resident.apartment_number;
    v_res_name := COALESCE(v_resident.full_name, 'الساكن / المالك');

    IF v_apt_id IS NULL AND v_apt_number IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT id INTO v_apt_id
        FROM public.apartments
        WHERE building_id = v_resident.building_id AND trim(apartment_number) = trim(v_apt_number)
        LIMIT 1;
    END IF;

    IF v_apt_id IS NULL AND v_apt_number IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Resident must be assigned to an apartment unit to submit payment.';
    END IF;

    -- 7. Validate emergency claim if category is emergency_claim
    IF p_payment_category = 'emergency_claim' THEN
        IF p_emergency_claim_id IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'معرف المطالبة الطارئة مطلوب لسداد بنود الطوارئ.';
        END IF;

        SELECT * INTO v_claim
        FROM public.emergency_claims
        WHERE id = p_emergency_claim_id;

        IF NOT FOUND OR v_claim.building_id <> v_resident.building_id THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'المطالبة الطارئة غير موجودة أو لا تخص هذه العمارة.';
        END IF;

        IF v_claim.status <> 'active' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'المطالبة الطارئة لم تعد نشطة (حالتها: %).', v_claim.status;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- 8. Payment method rules & receipt image url path validation
    IF p_payment_method = 'electronic_transfer' THEN
        IF COALESCE((v_building.features_config->>'receipt_upload')::boolean, true) = false THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'ميزة رفع إيصالات التحويل غير مفعلة في باقة العمارة الحالية.';
        END IF;

        v_receipt := trim(COALESCE(p_receipt_image_url, ''));
        IF v_receipt = '' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'يرجى إرفاق صورة إيصال التحويل الإلكتروني.';
        END IF;

        -- Path must reside within the building's designated bucket path
        IF NOT (
            v_receipt LIKE '%' || v_resident.building_id::text || '%'
            OR v_receipt LIKE 'payment-receipts/' || v_resident.building_id::text || '%'
        ) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'مسار إيصال السداد غير مصرح به أو لا يتبع هذه العمارة.';
        END IF;

        -- Prevent path traversal
        IF v_receipt LIKE '%..%' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'مسار الإيصال غير صالح.';
        END IF;
    ELSIF p_payment_method = 'cash' THEN
        IF p_cash_recipient IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'يرجى تحديد مستلم المبلغ النقدي (رئيس الاتحاد أو حارس العمارة).';
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- 9. Prevent duplicate pending or paid payment
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.payments
        WHERE (resident_id = v_caller_id OR (v_apt_id IS NOT NULL AND apartment_id = v_apt_id))
          AND building_id = v_resident.building_id
          AND payment_category = p_payment_category
          AND trim(billing_month) = v_month
          AND COALESCE(emergency_claim_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::uuid) =
              COALESCE(p_emergency_claim_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::uuid)
          AND status IN ('pending', 'paid')
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'تم سداد أو تقديم طلب سداد بالفعل لهذا الشهر/المطالبة. لا يمكن تكرار المعاملة.';
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 10. Insert payment record
    INSERT INTO public.payments (
        building_id,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        resident_id,
        resident_name,
        payment_category,
        emergency_claim_id,
        billing_month,
        amount,
        payment_method,
        cash_recipient,
        receipt_image_url,
        reference_number,
        notes,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        v_resident.building_id,
        v_apt_id,
        v_apt_number,
        v_caller_id,
        v_res_name,
        p_payment_category,
        p_emergency_claim_id,
        v_month,
        p_amount,
        p_payment_method,
        p_cash_recipient,
        v_receipt,
        p_reference_number,
        p_notes,
        'pending'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_payment_id;

    -- 11. Mark emergency claim share as pending
    IF p_payment_category = 'emergency_claim' AND p_emergency_claim_id IS NOT NULL AND v_apt_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE public.emergency_claim_shares
        SET status = 'pending'
        WHERE claim_id = p_emergency_claim_id
          AND apartment_id = v_apt_id
          AND status = 'unpaid';
    END IF;

    -- 12. Notify building admin
    IF v_building.admin_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO public.notifications (
            building_id,
            recipient_id,
            recipient_role,
            title,
            body,
            category,
            metadata
        ) VALUES (
            v_building.id,
            v_building.admin_id,
            'admin',
            'طلب اعتماد سداد جديد',
            format('قام الساكن (%s - شقة %s) بإرسال طلب سداد بمبلغ %s ج.م بانتظار المراجعة.',
                v_res_name, COALESCE(v_apt_number, '-'), p_amount),
            'financial',
            jsonb_build_object(
                'apartment_number', v_apt_number,
                'amount', p_amount,
                'payment_id', v_payment_id,
                'category', p_payment_category
            )
        );
    END IF;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'payment_id', v_payment_id,
        'status', 'pending',
        'amount', p_amount,
        'apartment_number', v_apt_number,
        'resident_name', v_res_name
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.submit_resident_payment(numeric, public.payment_method_type, text, public.cash_recipient_type, text, public.payment_category_type, uuid, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.submit_resident_payment(numeric, public.payment_method_type, text, public.cash_recipient_type, text, public.payment_category_type, uuid, text, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. RPC: create_emergency_claim
--    Only active building admin can create emergency claims.
--    Validates feature flags, positive amounts, and custom shares sum equality.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.create_emergency_claim(text, text, text, numeric, public.claim_distribution_type, jsonb, date, numeric, boolean, uuid, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.create_emergency_claim(text, text, text, numeric, public.claim_distribution_type, jsonb, date, numeric, boolean);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_emergency_claim(
    p_title TEXT,
    p_description TEXT,
    p_category TEXT,
    p_target_amount NUMERIC(12, 2),
    p_distribution_type public.claim_distribution_type DEFAULT 'equal',
    p_custom_shares JSONB DEFAULT NULL,
    p_due_date DATE DEFAULT NULL,
    p_default_share_amount NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT NULL,
    p_occupied_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_claim_id UUID;
    v_apt_count INTEGER;
    v_equal_share NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_share_item JSONB;
    v_share_apt_id UUID;
    v_share_amount NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_custom_sum NUMERIC(12, 2) := 0.00;
    v_apt_record RECORD;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can create emergency claims.';
    END IF;

    IF v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Admin is not assigned to an active building.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_admin.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_admin.building_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Building not found.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Feature flag verification
    IF COALESCE((v_building.features_config->>'emergency_claims')::boolean, true) = false THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'ميزة المطالبات الطارئة غير مفعلة في باقة العمارة الحالية.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Target amount validation
    IF p_target_amount IS NULL OR p_target_amount <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Target amount must be greater than zero.';
    END IF;

    -- 5. Equal distribution calculation
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_apt_count
    FROM public.apartments
    WHERE building_id = v_building.id AND is_active = true;

    v_equal_share := COALESCE(
        p_default_share_amount,
        CASE
            WHEN p_distribution_type = 'equal' THEN
                ROUND((p_target_amount / GREATEST(
                    CASE WHEN p_occupied_only THEN (
                        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.apartments 
                        WHERE building_id = v_building.id AND is_active = true 
                          AND (resident_id IS NOT NULL OR (occupant_name IS NOT NULL AND occupant_name <> '' AND occupant_name NOT IN ('وحدة شاغرة', 'شاغرة')))
                    ) ELSE v_apt_count END,
                    1
                ))::numeric, 2)
            ELSE NULL
        END
    );

    -- 6. Custom distribution validation: positive shares & sum equal target_amount
    IF p_distribution_type = 'custom' THEN
        IF p_custom_shares IS NULL OR jsonb_array_length(p_custom_shares) = 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Custom shares list is required when distribution type is custom.';
        END IF;

        FOR v_share_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_custom_shares)
        LOOP
            v_share_apt_id := (v_share_item->>'apartment_id')::uuid;
            v_share_amount := COALESCE((v_share_item->>'amount')::numeric, (v_share_item->>'assigned_amount')::numeric, 0.00);

            IF v_share_apt_id IS NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Apartment ID is required for each custom share.';
            END IF;

            IF v_share_amount <= 0 THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Custom share amount for apartment % must be greater than zero.', v_share_apt_id;
            END IF;

            SELECT * INTO v_apt_record
            FROM public.apartments
            WHERE id = v_share_apt_id AND building_id = v_building.id;

            IF NOT FOUND THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Apartment % does not belong to building %.', v_share_apt_id, v_building.id;
            END IF;

            v_custom_sum := v_custom_sum + v_share_amount;
        END LOOP;

        IF ROUND(v_custom_sum, 2) <> ROUND(p_target_amount, 2) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'مجموع حصص الشقق المخصصة (% ج.م) يجب أن يساوي المبلغ المستهدف للمطالبة (% ج.م) تماماً.', v_custom_sum, p_target_amount;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 7. Insert emergency claim record
    INSERT INTO public.emergency_claims (
        building_id,
        created_by,
        title,
        description,
        category,
        target_amount,
        collected_amount,
        contributors_count,
        distribution_type,
        default_share_amount,
        due_date,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        v_building.id,
        v_caller_id,
        trim(p_title),
        p_description,
        COALESCE(p_category, 'صيانة طارئة'),
        p_target_amount,
        0.00,
        0,
        p_distribution_type,
        v_equal_share,
        p_due_date,
        'active'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_claim_id;

    -- 8. Distribute shares across units
    IF p_distribution_type = 'equal' THEN
        INSERT INTO public.emergency_claim_shares (
            claim_id,
            building_id,
            apartment_id,
            resident_id,
            assigned_amount,
            paid_amount,
            status
        )
        SELECT
            v_claim_id,
            v_building.id,
            a.id,
            a.resident_id,
            v_equal_share,
            0.00,
            'unpaid'
        FROM public.apartments a
        WHERE a.building_id = v_building.id AND a.is_active = true
          AND (NOT p_occupied_only OR a.resident_id IS NOT NULL OR (a.occupant_name IS NOT NULL AND a.occupant_name <> '' AND a.occupant_name NOT IN ('وحدة شاغرة', 'شاغرة')));
    ELSIF p_distribution_type = 'custom' THEN
        FOR v_share_item IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(p_custom_shares)
        LOOP
            v_share_apt_id := (v_share_item->>'apartment_id')::uuid;
            v_share_amount := COALESCE((v_share_item->>'amount')::numeric, (v_share_item->>'assigned_amount')::numeric, 0.00);

            SELECT resident_id INTO v_apt_record
            FROM public.apartments
            WHERE id = v_share_apt_id;

            INSERT INTO public.emergency_claim_shares (
                claim_id,
                building_id,
                apartment_id,
                resident_id,
                assigned_amount,
                paid_amount,
                status
            ) VALUES (
                v_claim_id,
                v_building.id,
                v_share_apt_id,
                v_apt_record.resident_id,
                v_share_amount,
                0.00,
                'unpaid'
            );
        END LOOP;
    END IF;

    -- 9. Notify residents
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    ) VALUES (
        v_building.id,
        'resident',
        'مطالبة صيانة طارئة جديدة 🚨',
        format('تم فتح مطالبة صيانة طارئة: "%s" بمبلغ إجمالي %s ج.م.', trim(p_title), p_target_amount),
        'financial',
        jsonb_build_object(
            'claim_id', v_claim_id,
            'target_amount', p_target_amount,
            'distribution_type', p_distribution_type
        )
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'claim_id', v_claim_id,
        'building_id', v_building.id,
        'target_amount', p_target_amount,
        'distribution_type', p_distribution_type,
        'default_share_amount', v_equal_share,
        'status', 'active'
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_emergency_claim(text, text, text, numeric, public.claim_distribution_type, jsonb, date, numeric, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_emergency_claim(text, text, text, numeric, public.claim_distribution_type, jsonb, date, numeric, boolean) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 7. RPC: create_building_poll
--    Only active building admin can create general assembly polls.
--    Enforces voting feature flag, building writability, and admin building match.
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.create_building_poll(uuid, text, text, text, numeric, numeric, numeric, integer, jsonb, text, boolean);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_building_poll(
    p_building_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_title TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_description TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_category TEXT DEFAULT 'عام',
    p_estimated_cost NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    p_share_per_unit NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    p_quorum_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) DEFAULT 51.00,
    p_duration_days INTEGER DEFAULT 7,
    p_options JSONB DEFAULT NULL,
    p_document_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_is_urgent BOOLEAN DEFAULT false
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_poll_id UUID;
    v_session_code TEXT;
    v_options JSONB;
    v_target_bld_id UUID;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, role, status INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can create polls.';
    END IF;

    IF v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Admin is not assigned to an active building.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Verify target building matches caller's building
    IF p_building_id IS NOT NULL AND p_building_id <> v_admin.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: You cannot create polls for a different building.';
    END IF;

    v_target_bld_id := v_admin.building_id;

    -- 3. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_target_bld_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Check voting feature flag
    SELECT * INTO v_building FROM public.buildings WHERE id = v_target_bld_id;
    IF COALESCE((v_building.features_config->>'voting')::boolean, true) = false THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'ميزة التصويت وقرارات الجمعية العمومية غير مفعلة في باقة العمارة الحالية.';
    END IF;

    IF p_title IS NULL OR trim(p_title) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Poll title is required.';
    END IF;

    v_session_code := '#' || to_char(CURRENT_DATE, 'YY') || '-DEC-' || upper(substr(replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 1, 4));

    IF p_options IS NULL OR jsonb_array_length(p_options) = 0 THEN
        v_options := '[{"id":"approve","title":"موافق على القرار"},{"id":"reject","title":"رافض أو متحفظ"},{"id":"abstain","title":"ممتنع عن التصويت"}]'::jsonb;
    ELSE
        v_options := p_options;
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 5. Insert poll record
    INSERT INTO public.polls (
        building_id,
        created_by,
        session_code,
        title,
        description,
        category,
        estimated_cost,
        share_per_unit,
        quorum_percent,
        ends_at,
        options,
        document_url,
        is_urgent,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        v_target_bld_id,
        v_caller_id,
        v_session_code,
        trim(p_title),
        trim(p_description),
        COALESCE(p_category, 'عام'),
        COALESCE(p_estimated_cost, 0.00),
        COALESCE(p_share_per_unit, 0.00),
        COALESCE(p_quorum_percent, 51.00),
        now() + (COALESCE(p_duration_days, 7) || ' days')::interval,
        v_options,
        p_document_url,
        COALESCE(p_is_urgent, false),
        'active'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_poll_id;

    -- 6. Broadcast notification to residents
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    ) VALUES (
        v_target_bld_id,
        'resident',
        'تصويت وقرار جديد بانتظار مشاركتك 🗳️',
        'طرحت إدارة العمارة قراراً جديداً للتصويت: "' || trim(p_title) || '". صوتك يمثل شقتك ويوثق قانونياً.',
        'voting',
        jsonb_build_object('poll_id', v_poll_id, 'session_code', v_session_code)
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'poll_id', v_poll_id,
        'session_code', v_session_code,
        'title', trim(p_title)
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_building_poll(uuid, text, text, text, numeric, numeric, numeric, integer, jsonb, text, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_building_poll(uuid, text, text, text, numeric, numeric, numeric, integer, jsonb, text, boolean) TO authenticated;


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\009_tighten_rls_policies.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 009_tighten_rls_policies.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Step 3 Security Overhaul:
--              1. Tighten open RLS policies on users, buildings, apartments, notifications.
--                 - Remove any TO anon or USING (true) policies.
--                 - Restrict SELECT to authenticated users based on verified membership.
--                 - Protect sensitive fields (push_token, email, phone, arrears_amount).
--              2. Re-author lookup_building_apartments_for_join with auth.uid() check,
--                 masked occupant names (first 2 characters), phone matching boolean,
--                 and zero leakage of phone numbers or arrears.
--              3. Granular Realtime publication per table.
--              4. Restrict storage access for payment-receipts to owner and building admin.
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Tighten RLS: public.users
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_select_policy" ON public.users;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_select_authenticated" ON public.users;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "users_select_all" ON public.users;

-- Direct table select: Self, Admin of the same building, or Super Admin
CREATE POLICY "users_select_policy"
    ON public.users
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR id = auth.uid()
        OR (
            public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        )
    );

-- Secure View for building resident directory (Name & Unit only, NO push_token, email, or phone)
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.building_resident_directory
WITH (security_invoker = false)
AS
SELECT
    u.id,
    u.building_id,
    u.apartment_id,
    u.apartment_number,
    u.floor_number,
    u.full_name,
    u.role,
    u.status,
    u.residency_type,
    u.ownership_share_percent,
    u.created_at
FROM public.users u
WHERE u.status = 'active'
  AND (
      public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
      OR u.building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
  );

REVOKE ALL ON public.building_resident_directory FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT SELECT ON public.building_resident_directory TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. Tighten RLS: public.buildings
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "buildings_select_policy" ON public.buildings;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "buildings_select_authenticated" ON public.buildings;

-- Only members of the building or Super Admin can read building details
CREATE POLICY "buildings_select_policy"
    ON public.buildings
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. Tighten RLS: public.apartments
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartments_select_policy" ON public.apartments;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartments_select_authenticated" ON public.apartments;

CREATE POLICY "apartments_select_policy"
    ON public.apartments
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        )
    );

-- Secure View for apartments directory masking occupant phone and arrears for non-owners/non-admins
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.building_apartments_directory
WITH (security_invoker = false)
AS
SELECT
    a.id,
    a.building_id,
    a.apartment_number,
    a.floor_number,
    a.wing_or_direction,
    a.ownership_share_percent,
    a.resident_id,
    a.is_active,
    a.created_at,
    a.updated_at,
    CASE
        WHEN public.get_my_role() IN ('admin', 'super_admin') OR a.resident_id = auth.uid() THEN a.occupant_name
        WHEN a.occupant_name IS NOT NULL AND length(a.occupant_name) > 2 THEN substring(a.occupant_name from 1 for 2) || '***'
        ELSE a.occupant_name
    END AS occupant_name,
    CASE
        WHEN public.get_my_role() IN ('admin', 'super_admin') OR a.resident_id = auth.uid() THEN a.occupant_phone
        ELSE NULL
    END AS occupant_phone,
    CASE
        WHEN public.get_my_role() IN ('admin', 'super_admin') OR a.resident_id = auth.uid() THEN a.arrears_amount
        ELSE 0.00
    END AS arrears_amount
FROM public.apartments a
WHERE a.building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
  AND public.get_my_status() = 'active';

REVOKE ALL ON public.building_apartments_directory FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT SELECT ON public.building_apartments_directory TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. Tighten RLS: public.notifications
-- ============================================================================
-- Ensure notifications table supports broadcast recipient_role and metadata
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ALTER COLUMN recipient_id DROP NOT NULL;
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recipient_role TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "notifications_select_policy" ON public.notifications;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "notifications_select_own" ON public.notifications;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "notifications_update_own" ON public.notifications;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "notifications_update_policy" ON public.notifications;

-- Only recipient or users matching role and building can read notifications
CREATE POLICY "notifications_select_policy"
    ON public.notifications
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR recipient_id = auth.uid()
        OR (
            recipient_id IS NULL
            AND recipient_role = public.get_my_role()::text
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        )
    );

-- Users can only update their own notifications
CREATE POLICY "notifications_update_policy"
    ON public.notifications
    FOR UPDATE
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        recipient_id = auth.uid()
        OR (
            recipient_id IS NULL
            AND recipient_role = public.get_my_role()::text
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        recipient_id = auth.uid()
        OR (
            recipient_id IS NULL
            AND recipient_role = public.get_my_role()::text
            AND building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        )
    );

-- Enforce that ONLY is_read column can be updated by clients on notifications
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_notification_update_columns()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF (OLD.id <> NEW.id OR 
        OLD.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.building_id OR
        OLD.recipient_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.recipient_id OR
        OLD.recipient_role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.recipient_role OR
        OLD.title <> NEW.title OR
        OLD.body <> NEW.body OR
        OLD.category <> NEW.category OR
        OLD.metadata IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.metadata OR
        OLD.created_at <> NEW.created_at) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Only the is_read column can be modified on notifications.';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_restrict_notification_updates ON public.notifications;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_restrict_notification_updates
BEFORE UPDATE ON public.notifications
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.check_notification_update_columns();

-- Revoke write permissions from public and anon
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.notifications FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. RPC: lookup_building_apartments_for_join (Secured & Masked)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(
    p_building_code TEXT,
    p_phone TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_clean_phone TEXT;
    v_apartments JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_suggested_apt_id UUID := NULL;
    v_suggested_apt_number TEXT := NULL;
    v_suggested_occupant_name TEXT := NULL;
    v_suggested_floor_number TEXT := NULL;
    v_apt RECORD;
    v_is_match BOOLEAN;
    v_raw_name TEXT;
    v_masked_name TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Require Authenticated Session
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Find building by building_code or apartment invite_code
    SELECT id, name, address, building_code, total_apartments, default_monthly_dues
    INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE upper(trim(building_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code));

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        SELECT b.id, b.name, b.address, b.building_code, b.total_apartments, b.default_monthly_dues,
               a.id as apt_id, a.apartment_number,
               COALESCE(a.occupant_name, u.full_name) as occupant_name,
               COALESCE(a.floor_number, u.floor_number) as floor_number
        INTO v_apt
        FROM public.apartments a
        JOIN public.buildings b ON b.id = a.building_id
        LEFT JOIN public.users u ON u.id = a.resident_id OR (u.building_id = a.building_id AND u.apartment_number = a.apartment_number AND u.status = 'active')
        WHERE upper(trim(a.invite_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code))
        LIMIT 1;

        IF FOUND THEN
            v_building.id := v_apt.id;
            v_building.name := v_apt.name;
            v_building.address := v_apt.address;
            v_building.building_code := v_apt.building_code;
            v_building.total_apartments := v_apt.total_apartments;
            v_building.default_monthly_dues := v_apt.default_monthly_dues;
            v_suggested_apt_id := v_apt.apt_id;
            v_suggested_apt_number := v_apt.apartment_number;
            v_raw_name := v_apt.occupant_name;
            IF v_raw_name IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(v_raw_name)) > 2 THEN
                v_suggested_occupant_name := substring(trim(v_raw_name) from 1 for 2) || '****';
            ELSE
                v_suggested_occupant_name := v_raw_name;
            END IF;
            v_suggested_floor_number := v_apt.floor_number;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    IF v_building.id IS NULL AND p_building_code LIKE '%-%-%' THEN
        SELECT id, name, address, building_code, total_apartments, default_monthly_dues
        INTO v_building
        FROM public.buildings
        WHERE upper(trim(building_code)) = upper(trim(split_part(p_building_code, '-', 1) || '-' || split_part(p_building_code, '-', 2)));

        IF FOUND THEN
            v_suggested_apt_number := split_part(p_building_code, '-', 3);
        END IF;
    END IF;

    IF v_building.id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'كود المبنى غير صحيح، يرجى التأكد من الكود من رئيس الاتحاد.';
    END IF;

    v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(COALESCE(p_phone, ''), '[^0-9]', '', 'g');

    -- 3. Iterate through units: strictly mask name and NEVER leak phone or arrears
    FOR v_apt IN
        SELECT
            a.id,
            a.apartment_number,
            COALESCE(
                a.floor_number,
                u.floor_number,
                CASE
                    WHEN a.apartment_number ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND length(a.apartment_number) >= 2 THEN 'الدور ' || substring(a.apartment_number from 1 for length(a.apartment_number) - 1)
                    WHEN a.apartment_number ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN 'الدور الأول'
                    ELSE NULL
                END
            ) as floor_number,
            a.wing_or_direction,
            COALESCE(a.occupant_name, u.full_name) as raw_occupant_name,
            COALESCE(a.occupant_phone, u.phone) as raw_occupant_phone,
            a.resident_id
        FROM public.apartments a
        LEFT JOIN public.users u ON u.id = a.resident_id OR (u.building_id = a.building_id AND u.apartment_number = a.apartment_number AND u.status = 'active')
        WHERE a.building_id = v_building.id
          AND a.is_active = true
        ORDER BY a.apartment_number
    LOOP
        -- Matching is done server-side: returns boolean only
        v_is_match := (
            v_clean_phone <> ''
            AND length(v_clean_phone) >= 8
            AND regexp_replace(COALESCE(v_apt.raw_occupant_phone, ''), '[^0-9]', '', 'g') = v_clean_phone
        );

        -- Mask occupant name (first 2 chars + '****')
        IF v_apt.raw_occupant_name IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(v_apt.raw_occupant_name)) > 2 THEN
            v_masked_name := substring(trim(v_apt.raw_occupant_name) from 1 for 2) || '****';
        ELSE
            v_masked_name := v_apt.raw_occupant_name;
        END IF;

        IF v_suggested_apt_number IS NOT NULL AND v_apt.apartment_number = v_suggested_apt_number THEN
            v_suggested_apt_id := v_apt.id;
            v_suggested_occupant_name := v_masked_name;
            v_suggested_floor_number := v_apt.floor_number;
        END IF;

        IF v_is_match AND v_suggested_apt_id IS NULL THEN
            v_suggested_apt_id := v_apt.id;
            v_suggested_apt_number := v_apt.apartment_number;
            v_suggested_occupant_name := v_masked_name;
            v_suggested_floor_number := v_apt.floor_number;
        END IF;

        -- Safe payload: NO occupant_phone and NO arrears_amount
        v_apartments := v_apartments || jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_apt.id,
            'apartment_number', v_apt.apartment_number,
            'floor_number', v_apt.floor_number,
            'wing_or_direction', v_apt.wing_or_direction,
            'occupant_name', v_masked_name,
            'is_phone_match', v_is_match,
            'is_occupied', (v_apt.resident_id IS NOT NULL)
        );
    END LOOP;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'building_id', v_building.id,
        'building_name', v_building.name,
        'building_code', v_building.building_code,
        'suggested_apartment_id', v_suggested_apt_id,
        'suggested_apartment_number', v_suggested_apt_number,
        'suggested_occupant_name', v_suggested_occupant_name,
        'suggested_floor_number', v_suggested_floor_number,
        'apartments', v_apartments
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(text, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. Storage Security: payment-receipts restricted to owner & building admin
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "storage_building_members_select" ON storage.objects;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "storage_payment_receipts_select" ON storage.objects;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "storage_general_select" ON storage.objects;

-- Policy 1: payment-receipts is restricted to receipt owner, building admin, or super_admin
CREATE POLICY "storage_payment_receipts_select"
    ON storage.objects
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        bucket_id = 'payment-receipts'
        AND (
            public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
            OR (
                public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
                AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
                AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = public.get_my_building_id()::text
            )
            OR (
                -- Receipt owner: path includes user id or linked to user's payment row
                name LIKE '%' || auth.uid()::text || '%'
                OR (storage.foldername(name))[2] = auth.uid()::text
                OR EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM public.payments p
                    WHERE p.resident_id = auth.uid()
                      AND (p.receipt_image_url LIKE '%' || name || '%' OR name LIKE '%' || p.id::text || '%')
                )
            )
        )
    );

-- Policy 2: Other building files (expense invoices, documents, tickets)
CREATE POLICY "storage_general_select"
    ON storage.objects
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        bucket_id IN ('expense-invoices', 'subscription-receipts', 'building-documents', 'issue-attachments')
        AND (
            public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
            OR (
                (storage.foldername(name))[1] = public.get_my_building_id()::text
                AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            )
        )
    );

ALTER TABLE public.payments REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.expenses REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
ALTER TABLE public.emergency_claims REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.users;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.apartments;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;

    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.notifications;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.payments;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.expenses;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.emergency_claims;
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; WHEN undefined_object THEN NULL; END $$;


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\010_restrict_direct_table_writes.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 010_restrict_direct_table_writes.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Step 4 Security Overhaul:
--              1. poll_votes: Remove FOR ALL / direct insert policies. Writing via cast_poll_vote ONLY.
--                 Update cast_poll_vote: validates option choices, applies voting_basis weight,
--                 documents decision that votes are editable only before deadline (ends_at).
--              2. users: Prevent direct updates to apartment_id, status, role, building_id.
--                 Residents restricted to full_name and push_token only via trigger.
--              3. issue_upvotes: Remove FOR ALL policy. Writing via toggle_issue_upvote ONLY.
--              4. building_issues: Restrict direct INSERT to valid unit owner/open status;
--                 direct UPDATE prohibited (state updates strictly via update_issue_status RPC).
--              5. payments: Remove direct client INSERT/UPDATE. All mutations via submit/approve/reject RPCs.
--                 Add partial unique indexes to prevent concurrent duplicate pending requests.
--              6. expenses: Remove direct client INSERT. All mutations via record_building_expense / create_expense
--                 with FOR UPDATE building fund lock, positive amount, and negative balance protection.
--              7. apartments: Remove open FOR ALL policy. Add unique index on (building_id, apartment_number).
