--                 Split into disciplined INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE policies.
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 0. Schema Resilience: Ensure columns and tables exist
-- ============================================================================
-- Ensure poll and vote enhancements exist
ALTER TABLE public.poll_votes ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS apartment_number TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.poll_votes ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS voter_name TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.polls ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS options JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[{"id":"approve","title":"موافق على القرار"},{"id":"reject","title":"رافض أو متحفظ"},{"id":"abstain","title":"ممتنع عن التصويت"}]'::jsonb;
ALTER TABLE public.polls ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS votes_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE public.polls ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عام';

-- Ensure user columns exist
ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS push_token TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS push_token_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS floor_number TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS residency_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'owner_resident';
ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ownership_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2) DEFAULT 6.25;

-- Ensure expense columns exist
ALTER TABLE public.expenses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS expense_month TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT to_char(CURRENT_DATE, 'YYYY-MM');
ALTER TABLE public.expenses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS invoice_image_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.expenses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS invoice_number TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.expenses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS voucher_number TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.expenses ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_system_generated BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false;

-- Ensure building_issues and issue_upvotes exist if migration 007 was skipped or partial
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.building_issues (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    apartment_number TEXT,
    reporter_name TEXT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'المصعد',
    severity TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'medium',
    image_url TEXT,
    is_private BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    upvotes_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
    preferred_time TEXT DEFAULT 'any',
    whatsapp_notifications BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.issue_upvotes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    issue_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.building_issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.buildings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_id UUID REFERENCES public.apartments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    apartment_number TEXT NOT NULL,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Restrict poll_votes & Harden cast_poll_vote RPC
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "poll_votes_insert_resident" ON public.poll_votes;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "votes_insert_manage_policy" ON public.poll_votes;

-- Revoke direct write permissions from public/clients
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.poll_votes FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- Re-author cast_poll_vote RPC
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.cast_poll_vote(uuid, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cast_poll_vote(
    p_poll_id UUID,
    p_choice TEXT
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_poll RECORD;
    v_apt RECORD;
    v_total_apts INTEGER;
    v_weight NUMERIC(5, 2);
    v_votes_count INTEGER;
    v_choice_valid BOOLEAN := false;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify voter strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, apartment_id, apartment_number, full_name, role, status
    INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: User profile is not active.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Lock poll row FOR UPDATE and verify active status and deadline
    SELECT * INTO v_poll
    FROM public.polls
    WHERE id = p_poll_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Poll not found.';
    END IF;

    IF v_poll.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'This poll is no longer active.';
    END IF;

    -- DECISION ON VOTE EDITABILITY:
    -- A resident's vote is mutable (can be changed) strictly until the poll deadline (ends_at) passes.
    -- Once ends_at has passed or status is closed, votes are permanently locked and cannot be changed.
    IF v_poll.ends_at < now() THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'انتهى الموعد النهائي المحدد للتصويت. تم قفل الأصوات نهائياً.';
    END IF;

    IF v_poll.building_id <> v_user.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Poll belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Validate that p_choice exists in poll.options
    IF v_poll.options IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_array_length(v_poll.options) > 0 THEN
        SELECT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(v_poll.options) opt
            WHERE opt->>'id' = trim(p_choice)
               OR opt #>> '{}' = trim(p_choice)
        ) INTO v_choice_valid;

        IF NOT v_choice_valid THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'الخيار المحدد غير متاح ضمن خيارات هذا التصويت.';
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- 4. Calculate unit weight according to voting_basis
    SELECT total_apartments INTO v_total_apts
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_user.building_id;

    IF v_user.apartment_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT * INTO v_apt FROM public.apartments WHERE id = v_user.apartment_id;
    END IF;

    IF COALESCE(v_poll.voting_basis, 'ownership_share') = 'ownership_share' THEN
        -- Weight based on registered ownership share percentage
        v_weight := COALESCE(v_apt.ownership_share_percent, ROUND(100.0 / GREATEST(COALESCE(v_total_apts, 16), 1), 2));
    ELSE
        -- Equal vote per unit
        v_weight := ROUND(100.0 / GREATEST(COALESCE(v_total_apts, 16), 1), 2);
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 5. Upsert vote: strictly 1 vote per apartment unit
    INSERT INTO public.poll_votes (
        poll_id,
        building_id,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        voter_id,
        voter_name,
        choice,
        weight_percent
    ) VALUES (
        p_poll_id,
        v_user.building_id,
        COALESCE(v_user.apartment_id, v_caller_id),
        COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '1'),
        v_caller_id,
        COALESCE(v_user.full_name, 'ساكن'),
        trim(p_choice),
        v_weight
    )
    ON CONFLICT (poll_id, apartment_id)
    DO UPDATE SET
        choice = EXCLUDED.choice,
        voter_id = EXCLUDED.voter_id,
        voter_name = EXCLUDED.voter_name,
        weight_percent = EXCLUDED.weight_percent,
        created_at = now();

    -- 6. Recalculate and update votes_count
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_votes_count
    FROM public.poll_votes
    WHERE poll_id = p_poll_id;

    UPDATE public.polls
    SET votes_count = v_votes_count,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_poll_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'poll_id', p_poll_id,
        'choice', trim(p_choice),
        'apartment_number', v_user.apartment_number,
        'votes_count', v_votes_count,
        'weight_percent', v_weight
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cast_poll_vote(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cast_poll_vote(uuid, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. Restrict Direct Column Modifications on public.users
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_user_update_permissions()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_bypass TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Check if operation is running within a trusted SECURITY DEFINER RPC
    v_bypass := current_setting('app.rpc_internal_bypass', true);
    IF v_bypass = 'true' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Super Admin has full maintenance privileges
    v_caller_role := public.get_my_role();
    IF v_caller_role = 'super_admin' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- When a user updates their own profile directly
    IF auth.uid() = OLD.id THEN
        -- Prohibit tampering with security-critical columns
        IF (OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.role OR
            OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.status OR
            OLD.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.building_id OR
            OLD.apartment_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.apartment_id OR
            OLD.apartment_number IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.apartment_number OR
            OLD.ownership_share_percent IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.ownership_share_percent) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: You cannot modify role, status, building_id, or apartment assignments directly.';
        END IF;

        -- Regular resident can ONLY modify full_name, push_token, and push_token_updated_at directly
        IF OLD.role = 'resident' THEN
            IF (OLD.email IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.email OR
                OLD.phone IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.phone OR
                OLD.floor_number IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.floor_number OR
                OLD.residency_type IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.residency_type) THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Residents can only update full_name and push_token directly.';
            END IF;
        END IF;

        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Admin updating member of their building directly
    IF v_caller_role = 'admin' AND public.get_my_status() = 'active' AND OLD.building_id = public.get_my_building_id() THEN
        IF (OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.role OR
            OLD.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.building_id) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building admins cannot modify role or building_id of users directly.';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Unauthorized update on users table.';
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_check_user_update_permissions ON public.users;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_user_update_permissions
BEFORE UPDATE ON public.users
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.check_user_update_permissions();

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. Restrict issue_upvotes Direct Writes
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "upvotes_manage_policy" ON public.issue_upvotes;
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.issue_upvotes FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- Ensure toggle_issue_upvote has internal bypass configured
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.toggle_issue_upvote(
    p_issue_id UUID
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_user RECORD;
    v_issue RECORD;
    v_existing_id UUID;
    v_new_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, building_id, apartment_id, apartment_number, role, status
    INTO v_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_user IS NULL OR v_user.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User profile is not active.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_issue
    FROM public.building_issues
    WHERE id = p_issue_id;

    IF v_issue IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Issue not found.';
    END IF;

    IF v_issue.building_id <> v_user.building_id THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Issue belongs to a different building.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id INTO v_existing_id
    FROM public.issue_upvotes
    WHERE issue_id = p_issue_id
      AND (apartment_id = v_user.apartment_id OR (v_user.apartment_id IS NULL AND user_id = v_caller_id));

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    IF v_existing_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        DELETE FROM public.issue_upvotes WHERE id = v_existing_id;
        UPDATE public.building_issues
        SET upvotes_count = GREATEST(0, upvotes_count - 1),
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = p_issue_id
        RETURNING upvotes_count INTO v_new_count;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'action', 'removed',
            'upvoted', false,
            'upvotes_count', v_new_count
        );
    ELSE
        INSERT INTO public.issue_upvotes (
            issue_id,
            building_id,
            apartment_id,
            apartment_number,
            user_id
        ) VALUES (
            p_issue_id,
            v_user.building_id,
            v_user.apartment_id,
            COALESCE(v_user.apartment_number, '1'),
            v_caller_id
        );

        UPDATE public.building_issues
        SET upvotes_count = upvotes_count + 1,
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = p_issue_id
        RETURNING upvotes_count INTO v_new_count;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'action', 'added',
            'upvoted', true,
            'upvotes_count', v_new_count
        );
    END IF;
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.toggle_issue_upvote(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.toggle_issue_upvote(uuid) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. Restrict building_issues Direct Writes
-- ============================================================================
-- Direct UPDATE prohibited; status updates strictly managed via update_issue_status RPC
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "issues_update_policy" ON public.building_issues;
REVOKE UPDATE ON public.building_issues FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- Tighten direct INSERT to match user's verified unit and active state
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "issues_insert_policy" ON public.building_issues;
CREATE POLICY "issues_insert_policy"
    ON public.building_issues
    FOR INSERT
    TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (
        building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
        AND created_by = auth.uid()
        AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
        AND status = 'open'
        AND upvotes_count = 0
        AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
    );

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. Restrict payments Direct Writes & Enforce Partial Unique Indexes
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "payments_insert_resident" ON public.payments;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "payments_update_policy" ON public.payments;
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.payments FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- Partial unique indexes preventing concurrent duplicate pending requests
DO $$ BEGIN
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_payments_pending_monthly_dues
    ON public.payments (building_id, apartment_id, billing_month)
    WHERE status = 'pending' AND payment_category = 'monthly_dues';
EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN
    RAISE NOTICE 'Skipping index uq_payments_pending_monthly_dues due to existing duplicate records.';
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_payments_pending_emergency_claim
    ON public.payments (building_id, apartment_id, emergency_claim_id)
    WHERE status = 'pending' AND payment_category = 'emergency_claim';
EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN
    RAISE NOTICE 'Skipping index uq_payments_pending_emergency_claim due to existing duplicate records.';
END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. Restrict expenses Direct Writes & Create/Enforce Atomic RPC
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "expenses_insert_admin" ON public.expenses;
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.expenses FROM PUBLIC, anon;

-- Strengthen record_building_expense with FOR UPDATE lock & negative balance prevention
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.record_building_expense(
    p_title TEXT,
    p_amount NUMERIC(12, 2),
    p_category TEXT DEFAULT 'صيانة ومرافق',
    p_description TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_expense_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_invoice_image_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_invoice_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_voucher_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_allow_deficit BOOLEAN DEFAULT false
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_expense_id UUID;
    v_new_balance NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_date DATE;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id AND role = 'admin' AND status = 'active';

    IF NOT FOUND OR v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only an active building admin can record expenses.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Check building subscription writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_admin.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'انتهى اشتراك العمارة (وضع القراءة فقط Read-Only). يرجى تجديد الاشتراك لإضافة مصروفات جديدة.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Validate positive amount
    IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Expense amount must be greater than zero.';
    END IF;

    IF p_title IS NULL OR trim(p_title) = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Expense title is required.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Lock building row FOR UPDATE to protect fund_balance atomicity
    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_admin.building_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    -- Prevent negative fund_balance unless explicitly approved
    IF (v_building.fund_balance - p_amount) < 0 AND NOT COALESCE(p_allow_deficit, false) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'رصيد خزينة العمارة الحالي (% ج.م) لا يكفي لتغطية هذا المصروف (% ج.م).', v_building.fund_balance, p_amount;
    END IF;

    v_date := COALESCE(p_expense_date, CURRENT_DATE);

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 5. Insert expense record
    INSERT INTO public.expenses (
        building_id,
        created_by,
        title,
        description,
        category,
        amount,
        expense_date,
        expense_month,
        invoice_image_url,
        invoice_number,
        voucher_number,
        is_system_generated
    ) VALUES (
        v_admin.building_id,
        v_caller_id,
        trim(p_title),
        trim(p_description),
        COALESCE(p_category, 'صيانة ومرافق'),
        p_amount,
        v_date,
        to_char(v_date, 'YYYY-MM'),
        p_invoice_image_url,
        p_invoice_number,
        p_voucher_number,
        false
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_expense_id;

    -- 6. Atomically deduct fund_balance
    UPDATE public.buildings
    SET fund_balance = fund_balance - p_amount
    WHERE id = v_admin.building_id
    RETURNING fund_balance INTO v_new_balance;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'expense_id', v_expense_id,
        'deducted_amount', p_amount,
        'new_fund_balance', v_new_balance
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.record_building_expense(text, numeric, text, text, date, text, text, text, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.record_building_expense(text, numeric, text, text, date, text, text, text, boolean) TO authenticated;

-- Alias function create_expense matching common conventions
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_expense(
    p_title TEXT,
    p_amount NUMERIC(12, 2),
    p_category TEXT DEFAULT 'صيانة ومرافق',
    p_description TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_expense_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_invoice_image_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_invoice_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_voucher_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_allow_deficit BOOLEAN DEFAULT false
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN public.record_building_expense(
        p_title,
        p_amount,
        p_category,
        p_description,
        p_expense_date,
        p_invoice_image_url,
        p_invoice_number,
        p_voucher_number,
        p_allow_deficit
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_expense(text, numeric, text, text, date, text, text, text, boolean) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_expense(text, numeric, text, text, date, text, text, text, boolean) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 7. Restrict apartments Direct Writes & Prevent Duplicate Numbers
-- ============================================================================
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartments_manage_admin" ON public.apartments;

-- Prevent duplicate apartment unit numbers within the same building
DO $$ BEGIN
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_apartments_building_unit 
    ON public.apartments (building_id, trim(apartment_number));
EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN
    RAISE NOTICE 'Skipping index uq_apartments_building_unit due to existing duplicate records.';
END $$;

-- Disciplined INSERT: Only active building admin within building limits
CREATE POLICY "apartments_insert_admin"
    ON public.apartments
    FOR INSERT
    TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
            AND trim(apartment_number) <> ''
        )
    );

-- Disciplined UPDATE: Only active building admin; cannot mutate building_id
CREATE POLICY "apartments_update_admin"
    ON public.apartments
    FOR UPDATE
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
        )
    );

-- Disciplined DELETE: Only unassigned apartments with no active residents
CREATE POLICY "apartments_delete_admin"
    ON public.apartments
    FOR DELETE
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR (
            building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
            AND public.get_my_role() = 'admin'
            AND public.get_my_status() = 'active'
            AND public.is_building_writable(building_id)
            AND resident_id IS NULL
        )
    );


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\011_missing_rpcs_and_logic_fixes.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 011_missing_rpcs_and_logic_fixes.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Step 5 Security & Business Logic Hardening:
--              1. Drop unused legacy table maintenance_tickets (superseded by building_issues).
--              2. Rewrite request_join_building:
--                 - Requires auth.uid().
--                 - Removes split_part hack on building_code.
--                 - Checks if user is already an active member of another building.
--                 - Enforces max_apartments_override / subscription plan limits.
--              3. Rewrite create_building_for_admin:
--                 - Reads email safely from auth.jwt() or public.users (not raw auth.users).
--                 - Prevents admin from creating multiple buildings.
--                 - Promotes user profile to role = 'admin' and status = 'active'.
--              4. Add reject_app_subscription_invoice (Super Admin only, FOR UPDATE, notify admin).
--              5. Rewrite submit_app_subscription_invoice:
--                 - Active admin only, building strictly from DB, FOR UPDATE lock,
--                 - Validates receipt image, prevents duplicate pending invoices.
--              6. Rewrite send_bulk_unpaid_reminder:
--                 - Active admin only, checks notifications feature flag & writability,
--                 - Generates reminders for overdue apartments only for target month.
--              7. Enforce feature flags across all workflows.
-- ============================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- 0. Schema Resilience: Ensure notifications table supports role-based alerts
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ALTER COLUMN recipient_id DROP NOT NULL;
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recipient_role TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.notifications ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. Drop Legacy Unused maintenance_tickets Table
--    (Superseded by building_issues in 007_community_hub_issues_and_polls.sql)
-- ============================================================================
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS public.maintenance_tickets CASCADE;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. RPC: request_join_building (Secured & Enforced Limits)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.request_join_building(text, text, text, text, text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.request_join_building(
    p_full_name TEXT,
    p_phone TEXT,
    p_building_code TEXT,
    p_apartment_number TEXT,
    p_floor_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_residency_type TEXT DEFAULT 'owner_resident'
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_caller_email TEXT;
    v_existing_user RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_plan_max_apts INTEGER;
    v_effective_max_apts INTEGER;
    v_current_apts_count INTEGER;
    v_apartment_id UUID;
    v_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2);
BEGIN
    -- 1. Require Authenticated Session
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    -- Retrieve caller email safely without accessing private auth.users table directly
    v_caller_email := COALESCE(auth.jwt()->>'email', (SELECT email FROM public.users WHERE id = v_caller_id));

    -- 2. Verify caller is not already an active member of another building
    SELECT building_id, status, role INTO v_existing_user
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_existing_user.building_id IS NOT NULL AND v_existing_user.status = 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User is already an active member of an existing building.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Find building by exact building_code or apartment invite_code (NO split_part hacks)
    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE upper(trim(building_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code));

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        SELECT b.* INTO v_building
        FROM public.apartments a
        JOIN public.buildings b ON b.id = a.building_id
        WHERE upper(trim(a.invite_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code))
        LIMIT 1;
    END IF;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'كود المبنى غير صحيح، يرجى التأكد من الكود من رئيس الاتحاد.';
    END IF;

    -- 4. Check max apartments limit (building custom override vs plan tier)
    SELECT max_apartments INTO v_plan_max_apts
    FROM public.subscription_plans
    WHERE code = v_building.plan_code;

    v_effective_max_apts := COALESCE(v_building.max_apartments_override, v_plan_max_apts, 50);

    -- 5. Find or create apartment within plan limits
    SELECT id INTO v_apartment_id
    FROM public.apartments
    WHERE building_id = v_building.id
      AND trim(apartment_number) = trim(p_apartment_number);

    v_share_percent := ROUND((100.0 / GREATEST(v_building.total_apartments, 1))::numeric, 2);

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    IF v_apartment_id IS NULL THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_apts_count
        FROM public.apartments
        WHERE building_id = v_building.id AND is_active = true;

        IF v_current_apts_count >= v_effective_max_apts THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'تم الوصول للحد الأقصى للشقق المسموح به في باقة هذه العمارة (%).', v_effective_max_apts;
        END IF;

        INSERT INTO public.apartments (
            building_id,
            apartment_number,
            floor_number,
            ownership_share_percent,
            invite_code,
            is_active
        ) VALUES (
            v_building.id,
            trim(p_apartment_number),
            p_floor_number,
            v_share_percent,
            v_building.building_code || '-' || trim(p_apartment_number),
            true
        )
        RETURNING id INTO v_apartment_id;
    END IF;

    -- 6. Upsert user profile strictly as resident with status = 'pending'
    INSERT INTO public.users (
        id,
        full_name,
        email,
        phone,
        role,
        status,
        building_id,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        floor_number,
        residency_type,
        ownership_share_percent
    ) VALUES (
        v_caller_id,
        trim(p_full_name),
        v_caller_email,
        trim(p_phone),
        'resident',
        'pending',
        v_building.id,
        v_apartment_id,
        trim(p_apartment_number),
        p_floor_number,
        COALESCE(p_residency_type, 'owner_resident'),
        v_share_percent
    )
    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
        full_name = EXCLUDED.full_name,
        phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
        role = 'resident',
        status = 'pending',
        building_id = EXCLUDED.building_id,
        apartment_id = EXCLUDED.apartment_id,
        apartment_number = EXCLUDED.apartment_number,
        floor_number = EXCLUDED.floor_number,
        residency_type = EXCLUDED.residency_type;

    -- 7. Notify building admin
    IF v_building.admin_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO public.notifications (building_id, recipient_id, recipient_role, title, body, category)
        VALUES (
            v_building.id,
            v_building.admin_id,
            'admin',
            'طلب انضمام ساكن جديد',
            format('طلب الساكن (%s) الانضمام لشقة رقم (%s) وهو بانتظار موافقتك.', trim(p_full_name), trim(p_apartment_number)),
            'general'
        );
    END IF;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'user_id', v_caller_id,
        'building_id', v_building.id,
        'building_name', v_building.name,
        'apartment_id', v_apartment_id,
        'apartment_number', trim(p_apartment_number),
        'status', 'pending'
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.request_join_building(text, text, text, text, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.request_join_building(text, text, text, text, text, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. RPC: create_building_for_admin (Safe Caller Email & Role Promotion)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.create_building_for_admin(text, text, text, text, integer, numeric, text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_building_for_admin(
    p_full_name TEXT,
    p_phone TEXT,
    p_building_name TEXT,
    p_address TEXT,
    p_total_apartments INTEGER DEFAULT 16,
    p_default_monthly_dues NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 300.00,
    p_admin_apartment_number TEXT DEFAULT '1',
    p_registration_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_caller_email TEXT;
    v_existing_building UUID;
    v_building_id UUID;
    v_building_code TEXT;
    v_apartment_id UUID;
    v_pro_features JSONB;
    v_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2);
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    -- Retrieve caller email safely from JWT / public.users
    v_caller_email := COALESCE(auth.jwt()->>'email', (SELECT email FROM public.users WHERE id = v_caller_id));

    -- 2. Verify caller is not already assigned as admin of any building
    SELECT building_id INTO v_existing_building
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_existing_building IS NOT NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User is already assigned to a building.';
    END IF;

    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.buildings WHERE admin_id = v_caller_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User is already an administrator of a building.';
    END IF;

    IF p_total_apartments IS NULL OR p_total_apartments <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Total apartments must be greater than zero.';
    END IF;

    -- Fetch full Pro features from subscription_plans
    SELECT default_features_config INTO v_pro_features
    FROM public.subscription_plans
    WHERE code = 'pro';

    IF v_pro_features IS NULL THEN
        v_pro_features := '{
            "receipt_upload": true,
            "emergency_claims": true,
            "export_reports": true,
            "voting": true,
            "notifications": true,
            "documents_archive": true
        }'::jsonb;
    END IF;

    v_building_code := public.generate_unique_building_code();
    v_share_percent := ROUND((100.0 / p_total_apartments)::numeric, 2);

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 3. Create the building with a 60-day Pro trial (admin_id linked after user row exists)
    INSERT INTO public.buildings (
        name,
        address,
        registration_number,
        building_code,
        fund_balance,
        default_monthly_dues,
        total_apartments,
        plan_code,
        subscription_status,
        trial_ends_at,
        subscription_ends_at,
        features_config
    ) VALUES (
        trim(p_building_name),
        trim(p_address),
        p_registration_number,
        v_building_code,
        0.00,
        COALESCE(p_default_monthly_dues, 0.00),
        p_total_apartments,
        'pro',
        'trial',
        now() + INTERVAL '60 days',
        now() + INTERVAL '60 days',
        v_pro_features
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_building_id;

    -- 4. Create the admin's apartment unit (resident_id linked after user row exists)
    INSERT INTO public.apartments (
        building_id,
        apartment_number,
        ownership_share_percent,
        occupant_name,
        occupant_phone,
        invite_code,
        is_active
    ) VALUES (
        v_building_id,
        trim(p_admin_apartment_number),
        v_share_percent,
        trim(p_full_name),
        trim(p_phone),
        v_building_code || '-' || trim(p_admin_apartment_number),
        true
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_apartment_id;

    -- 5. Promote user to role = 'admin' and status = 'active' in public.users
    INSERT INTO public.users (
        id,
        full_name,
        email,
        phone,
        role,
        status,
        building_id,
        apartment_id,
        apartment_number,
        ownership_share_percent
    ) VALUES (
        v_caller_id,
        trim(p_full_name),
        v_caller_email,
        trim(p_phone),
        'admin',
        'active',
        v_building_id,
        v_apartment_id,
        trim(p_admin_apartment_number),
        v_share_percent
    )
    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
        full_name = EXCLUDED.full_name,
        email = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.email, public.users.email),
        phone = EXCLUDED.phone,
        role = 'admin',
        status = 'active',
        building_id = EXCLUDED.building_id,
        apartment_id = EXCLUDED.apartment_id,
        apartment_number = EXCLUDED.apartment_number,
        ownership_share_percent = EXCLUDED.ownership_share_percent;

    -- 6. Link building.admin_id and apartment.resident_id safely now that user exists
    UPDATE public.buildings SET admin_id = v_caller_id WHERE id = v_building_id;
    UPDATE public.apartments SET resident_id = v_caller_id WHERE id = v_apartment_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'building_id', v_building_id,
        'building_code', v_building_code,
        'apartment_id', v_apartment_id,
        'trial_ends_at', (now() + INTERVAL '60 days'),
        'plan_code', 'pro',
        'role', 'admin',
        'status', 'active'
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_building_for_admin(text, text, text, text, integer, numeric, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_building_for_admin(text, text, text, text, integer, numeric, text, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. RPC: reject_app_subscription_invoice (Super Admin only, FOR UPDATE)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.reject_app_subscription_invoice(uuid, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reject_app_subscription_invoice(
    p_invoice_id UUID,
    p_rejection_reason TEXT DEFAULT 'بيانات التحويل غير مطابقة أو لم يصل المبلغ'
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_invoice RECORD;
    v_reason TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify super_admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT role INTO v_caller_role
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id AND status = 'active';

    IF v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Only Super Admin can reject building subscriptions.';
    END IF;

    v_reason := trim(COALESCE(p_rejection_reason, ''));
    IF v_reason = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Rejection reason is required.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Lock the target invoice FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_invoice
    FROM public.app_subscription_invoices
    WHERE id = p_invoice_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'فاتورة الاشتراك غير موجودة.';
    END IF;

    IF v_invoice.status = 'rejected' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('invoice_id', p_invoice_id, 'status', 'rejected', 'message', 'Already rejected');
    END IF;

    IF v_invoice.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'منع التكرار المزدوج: حالة الفاتورة الحالية (%).', v_invoice.status;
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 3. Mark invoice as rejected
    UPDATE public.app_subscription_invoices
    SET status = 'rejected',
        notes = COALESCE(notes, '') || ' [سبب الرفض: ' || v_reason || ']'
    WHERE id = p_invoice_id;

    -- 4. Send official notification to building admin
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category,
        metadata
    ) VALUES (
        v_invoice.building_id,
        v_invoice.submitted_by,
        'admin',
        'تعذر اعتماد تجديد باقة العمارة ⚠️',
        format('تم رفض طلب تجديد الباقة بمبلغ %s ج.م. سبب الرفض: "%s". يرجى إعادة المحاولة من صفحة باقة واشتراك التطبيق.',
            v_invoice.amount, v_reason),
        'subscription',
        jsonb_build_object('invoice_id', p_invoice_id, 'reason', v_reason)
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'invoice_id', p_invoice_id,
        'status', 'rejected',
        'rejection_reason', v_reason
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reject_app_subscription_invoice(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reject_app_subscription_invoice(uuid, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 5. RPC: submit_app_subscription_invoice (Secured Building & Receipt Check)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.submit_app_subscription_invoice(public.plan_tier, integer, text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.submit_app_subscription_invoice(
    p_plan_code public.plan_tier,
    p_duration_months INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    p_receipt_image_url TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    p_notes TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_plan RECORD;
    v_monthly_price NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_total_amount NUMERIC(12, 2);
    v_invoice_id UUID;
    v_receipt TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only an active building admin can submit subscription invoices.';
    END IF;

    IF v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Admin is not assigned to a building.';
    END IF;

    v_receipt := trim(COALESCE(p_receipt_image_url, ''));
    IF v_receipt = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'صورة إيصال التحويل مطلوبة لتقديم طلب التجديد.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Lock building row FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_admin.building_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    -- 3. Prevent duplicate pending invoices
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.app_subscription_invoices
        WHERE building_id = v_admin.building_id AND status = 'pending'
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'يوجد طلب تجديد اشتراك قيد المراجعة بالفعل لهذه العمارة. يرجى انتظار رد الإدارة العليا.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_plan
    FROM public.subscription_plans
    WHERE code = p_plan_code;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Subscription plan not found.';
    END IF;

    v_monthly_price := COALESCE(v_building.custom_monthly_price, v_plan.default_monthly_price);
    v_total_amount := v_monthly_price * GREATEST(COALESCE(p_duration_months, 1), 1);

    IF v_total_amount <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن رفع إيصال سداد لباقة بقيمة صفر.';
    END IF;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 4. Insert subscription invoice
    INSERT INTO public.app_subscription_invoices (
        building_id,
        submitted_by,
        plan_code,
        amount,
        duration_months,
        receipt_image_url,
        notes,
        status
    ) VALUES (
        v_building.id,
        v_caller_id,
        p_plan_code,
        v_total_amount,
        GREATEST(COALESCE(p_duration_months, 1), 1),
        v_receipt,
        p_notes,
        'pending'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_invoice_id;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'invoice_id', v_invoice_id,
        'building_id', v_building.id,
        'plan_code', p_plan_code,
        'amount', v_total_amount,
        'status', 'pending'
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.submit_app_subscription_invoice(public.plan_tier, integer, text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.submit_app_subscription_invoice(public.plan_tier, integer, text, text) TO authenticated;

-- ============================================================================
-- 6. RPC: send_bulk_unpaid_reminder (Enforce Feature Flag & Unpaid Targeting)
-- ============================================================================
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.send_bulk_unpaid_reminder(text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.send_bulk_unpaid_reminder(
    p_billing_month TEXT,
    p_custom_message TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_unpaid_rec RECORD;
    v_title TEXT;
    v_body TEXT;
    v_reminded_count INTEGER := 0;
    v_push_targets JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_reminded_apartments JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_month TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required.';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' OR v_admin.building_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only the active building admin can send bulk payment reminders.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Check building writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_admin.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building subscription has expired or is in read-only mode.';
    END IF;

    SELECT * INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE id = v_admin.building_id;

    -- 3. Check notifications feature flag
    IF COALESCE((v_building.features_config->>'notifications')::boolean, true) = false THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'ميزة التنبيهات والإشعارات غير مفعلة في باقة العمارة الحالية.';
    END IF;

    v_month := trim(COALESCE(p_billing_month, ''));
    IF NOT (v_month ~ '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'صيغة الشهر غير صالحة. يجب أن تكون بصيغة YYYY-MM (مثال: 2026-05).';
    END IF;

    v_title := format('تذكير بسداد اشتراك شهر %s 🔔', v_month);

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 4. Target ONLY units that have NOT paid or submitted pending payment for target month
    FOR v_unpaid_rec IN
        SELECT
            a.id AS apartment_id,
            a.apartment_number,
            u.id AS resident_id,
            u.full_name,
            u.push_token
        FROM public.apartments a
        LEFT JOIN public.users u
            ON (u.id = a.resident_id OR (u.building_id = a.building_id AND trim(u.apartment_number) = trim(a.apartment_number)))
            AND u.status = 'active'
        WHERE a.building_id = v_admin.building_id
          AND a.is_active = true
          AND NOT EXISTS (
              SELECT 1
              FROM public.payments p
              WHERE p.building_id = v_admin.building_id
                AND p.apartment_id = a.id
                AND p.payment_category = 'monthly_dues'
                AND trim(p.billing_month) = v_month
                AND p.status IN ('paid', 'pending')
          )
        ORDER BY a.apartment_number
    LOOP
        v_body := COALESCE(
            NULLIF(trim(p_custom_message), ''),
            format(
                'مرحباً %s (شقة %s)، نود تذكيركم بسداد اشتراك الصيانة الشهري عن (%s) بقيمة %s ج.م لضمان انتظام خدمات العمارة.',
                COALESCE(v_unpaid_rec.full_name, 'ساكن الوحدة'),
                v_unpaid_rec.apartment_number,
                v_month,
                v_building.default_monthly_dues
            )
        );

        INSERT INTO public.notifications (
            building_id,
            recipient_id,
            title,
            body,
            category,
            metadata
        ) VALUES (
            v_admin.building_id,
            v_unpaid_rec.resident_id,
            v_title,
            v_body,
            'financial',
            jsonb_build_object(
                'apartment_number', v_unpaid_rec.apartment_number,
                'billing_month', v_month,
                'amount', v_building.default_monthly_dues
            )
        );

        v_reminded_count := v_reminded_count + 1;
        v_reminded_apartments := v_reminded_apartments || to_jsonb(v_unpaid_rec.apartment_number);

        IF v_unpaid_rec.push_token IS NOT NULL AND trim(v_unpaid_rec.push_token) <> '' THEN
            v_push_targets := v_push_targets || jsonb_build_object(
                'resident_id', v_unpaid_rec.resident_id,
                'apartment_number', v_unpaid_rec.apartment_number,
                'push_token', v_unpaid_rec.push_token,
                'title', v_title,
                'body', v_body
            );
        END IF;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'building_id', v_admin.building_id,
        'billing_month', v_month,
        'reminded_count', v_reminded_count,
        'reminded_apartments', v_reminded_apartments,
        'push_targets', v_push_targets
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.send_bulk_unpaid_reminder(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.send_bulk_unpaid_reminder(text, text) TO authenticated;


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\012_fix_join_and_lookup_permissions.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 012_fix_join_and_lookup_permissions.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Allow prospective residents to look up building apartments using 
--              the building invite code before having an active account.
--              1. Allow public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join for anon & authenticated.
--              2. Allow SELECT on public.buildings for anon & authenticated.
-- ============================================================================

-- 1. Ensure buildings table can be read by invite code for prospective residents
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "buildings_select_policy" ON public.buildings;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "buildings_select_authenticated" ON public.buildings;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "buildings_select_by_code" ON public.buildings;

CREATE POLICY "buildings_select_policy"
    ON public.buildings
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated, anon
    USING (true);

-- 2. Rewrite lookup_building_apartments_for_join to allow prospective users
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(text, text);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(
    p_building_code TEXT,
    p_phone TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_building RECORD;
    v_clean_phone TEXT;
    v_apartments JSONB := '[]'::jsonb;
    v_suggested_apt_id UUID := NULL;
    v_suggested_apt_number TEXT := NULL;
    v_suggested_occupant_name TEXT := NULL;
    v_suggested_floor_number TEXT := NULL;
    v_apt RECORD;
    v_is_match BOOLEAN;
    v_raw_name TEXT;
    v_masked_name TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Clean inputs
    IF p_phone IS NOT NULL THEN
        v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(p_phone, '[^0-9]', '', 'g');
        IF length(v_clean_phone) > 10 THEN
            v_clean_phone := right(v_clean_phone, 10);
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- 2. Find building by exact building_code or apartment invite_code
    SELECT id, name, address, building_code, total_apartments, default_monthly_dues
    INTO v_building
    FROM public.buildings
    WHERE upper(trim(building_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code));

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        SELECT b.id, b.name, b.address, b.building_code, b.total_apartments, b.default_monthly_dues
        INTO v_building
        FROM public.apartments a
        JOIN public.buildings b ON b.id = a.building_id
        WHERE upper(trim(a.invite_code)) = upper(trim(p_building_code))
        LIMIT 1;
    END IF;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'error', 'كود المبنى غير صحيح أو غير مسجل في قاعدة البيانات.'
        );
    END IF;

    -- 3. Fetch apartments for this building with safe occupant masking
    FOR v_apt IN
        SELECT 
            a.id,
            a.apartment_number,
            a.floor_number,
            a.wing_or_direction,
            a.occupant_name,
            a.occupant_phone,
            a.arrears_amount,
            (a.resident_id IS NOT NULL) AS is_occupied
        FROM public.apartments a
        WHERE a.building_id = v_building.id
          AND a.is_active = true
        ORDER BY 
            CASE WHEN a.apartment_number ~ '^\d+$' THEN a.apartment_number::INTEGER ELSE 99999 END,
            a.apartment_number
    LOOP
        v_is_match := false;
        IF v_clean_phone IS NOT NULL AND v_apt.occupant_phone IS NOT NULL THEN
            IF right(regexp_replace(v_apt.occupant_phone, '[^0-9]', '', 'g'), 10) = v_clean_phone THEN
                v_is_match := true;
                v_suggested_apt_id := v_apt.id;
                v_suggested_apt_number := v_apt.apartment_number;
                v_suggested_occupant_name := v_apt.occupant_name;
                v_suggested_floor_number := v_apt.floor_number;
            END IF;
        END IF;

        -- Safe name masking
        v_raw_name := COALESCE(v_apt.occupant_name, '');
        IF length(v_raw_name) > 3 THEN
            v_masked_name := left(v_raw_name, 2) || '***' || right(v_raw_name, 1);
        ELSE
            v_masked_name := v_raw_name;
        END IF;

        v_apartments := v_apartments || jsonb_build_object(
            'id', v_apt.id,
            'apartment_number', v_apt.apartment_number,
            'floor_number', v_apt.floor_number,
            'wing_or_direction', v_apt.wing_or_direction,
            'occupant_name', CASE WHEN v_is_match THEN v_apt.occupant_name ELSE v_masked_name END,
            'is_occupied', v_apt.is_occupied,
            'arrears_amount', v_apt.arrears_amount
        );
    END LOOP;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'building_id', v_building.id,
        'building_name', v_building.name,
        'building_code', v_building.building_code,
        'total_apartments', v_building.total_apartments,
        'default_monthly_dues', v_building.default_monthly_dues,
        'apartments', v_apartments,
        'suggested_apartment_id', v_suggested_apt_id,
        'suggested_apartment_number', v_suggested_apt_number,
        'suggested_occupant_name', v_suggested_occupant_name,
        'suggested_floor_number', v_suggested_floor_number
    );
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.lookup_building_apartments_for_join(text, text) TO authenticated, anon;


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\013_fix_resident_sync_and_approval.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 013_fix_resident_sync_and_approval.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: 
--   1. Allow pending residents to read apartments & obligations of their building.
--   2. Fix review_resident_join_request to overwrite apartment occupant_name & occupant_phone
--      with the approved resident's actual full_name and phone.
--   3. Auto-sync any existing approved resident names onto their apartments.
-- ============================================================================

-- 1. Update RLS: public.apartments (Allow building members, even if pending, to see building apartments)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartments_select_policy" ON public.apartments;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartments_select_authenticated" ON public.apartments;

CREATE POLICY "apartments_select_policy"
    ON public.apartments
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

-- 2. Update RLS: public.apartment_obligations (Allow building members to view their building obligations)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartment_obligations_select" ON public.apartment_obligations;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "apartment_obligations_select_authenticated" ON public.apartment_obligations;

CREATE POLICY "apartment_obligations_select"
    ON public.apartment_obligations
    FOR SELECT
    TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.get_my_role() = 'super_admin'
        OR building_id = public.get_my_building_id()
    );

-- 3. Fix review_resident_join_request RPC to update occupant_name with the resident's actual name
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(
    p_resident_user_id UUID,
    p_approve BOOLEAN,
    p_override_apartment_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_override_apartment_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_admin RECORD;
    v_resident RECORD;
    v_target_apt RECORD;
    v_final_apt_id UUID;
    v_final_apt_number TEXT;
    v_final_floor TEXT;
    v_new_status public.account_status;
BEGIN
    -- 1. Strict Identity Check via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    SELECT id, role, status, building_id INTO v_admin
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_admin.id IS NULL OR v_admin.role <> 'admin' OR v_admin.status <> 'active' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Only active building admins can review join requests.';
    END IF;

    -- 2. Validate Building Subscription / Writability
    IF NOT public.is_building_writable(v_admin.building_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Building subscription has expired (read-only mode). Cannot approve residents.';
    END IF;

    -- 3. Lock resident row FOR UPDATE
    SELECT * INTO v_resident
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = p_resident_user_id
      AND building_id = v_admin.building_id
    FOR UPDATE;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Pending resident not found or belongs to another building.';
    END IF;

    IF v_resident.status <> 'pending' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'message', 'Resident was already reviewed.',
            'status', v_resident.status
        );
    END IF;

    -- 4. Determine final apartment assignment
    v_final_apt_id := COALESCE(p_override_apartment_id, v_resident.apartment_id);
    v_final_apt_number := COALESCE(trim(p_override_apartment_number), v_resident.apartment_number);
    v_final_floor := v_resident.floor_number;

    IF p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL OR p_override_apartment_number IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT * INTO v_target_apt
        FROM public.apartments
        WHERE building_id = v_admin.building_id
          AND (
              (p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL AND id = p_override_apartment_id)
              OR
              (p_override_apartment_number IS NOT NULL AND apartment_number = trim(p_override_apartment_number))
          )
        LIMIT 1;

        IF FOUND THEN
            v_final_apt_id := v_target_apt.id;
            v_final_apt_number := v_target_apt.apartment_number;
            v_final_floor := COALESCE(v_target_apt.floor_number, v_resident.floor_number);
        ELSE
            IF p_override_apartment_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Specified override apartment does not exist in this building.';
            END IF;
            v_final_apt_number := trim(p_override_apartment_number);
        END IF;
    END IF;

    v_new_status := CASE WHEN p_approve THEN 'active'::public.account_status ELSE 'rejected'::public.account_status END;

    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 5. Update resident status and assigned unit
    UPDATE public.users
    SET status = v_new_status,
        apartment_id = v_final_apt_id,
        apartment_number = v_final_apt_number,
        floor_number = v_final_floor,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_resident_user_id;

    -- 6. If approved, bind resident to unit and OVERWRITE occupant_name with resident's actual name
    IF p_approve AND v_final_apt_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE public.apartments
        SET resident_id = p_resident_user_id,
            occupant_name = COALESCE(NULLIF(trim(v_resident.full_name), ''), occupant_name),
            occupant_phone = COALESCE(NULLIF(trim(v_resident.phone), ''), occupant_phone),
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = v_final_apt_id;
    END IF;

    -- 7. Send notification to resident
    INSERT INTO public.notifications (
        building_id,
        recipient_id,
        recipient_role,
        title,
        body,
        category
    ) VALUES (
        v_admin.building_id,
        p_resident_user_id,
        'resident',
        CASE WHEN p_approve THEN 'تم تفعيل حسابك بنجاح 🎉' ELSE 'تم رفض طلب الانضمام' END,
        CASE WHEN p_approve
            THEN format('تمت موافقة رئيس اتحاد الملاك على انضمامك وتسكينك على شقة رقم (%s). يمكنك الآن استخدام كافة خدمات التطبيق.', COALESCE(v_final_apt_number, '-'))
            ELSE 'نعتذر، لم تتم الموافقة على طلب انضمامك. يرجى التواصل مع رئيس اتحاد الملاك.'
        END,
        'join_request'
    );

    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', true,
        'status', v_new_status,
        'resident_id', p_resident_user_id,
        'apartment_id', v_final_apt_id,
        'apartment_number', v_final_apt_number
    );
END;
$$;

REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.review_resident_join_request(uuid, boolean, uuid, text) TO authenticated;

-- 4. One-time fix: Sync any existing approved resident name onto their apartment
UPDATE public.apartments a
SET occupant_name = u.full_name,
    occupant_phone = u.phone,
    updated_at = now()
FROM public.users u
WHERE a.resident_id = u.id
  AND u.status = 'active'
  AND u.full_name IS NOT NULL
  AND u.full_name <> '';


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\014_admin_reset_tools_and_cascade.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 014_admin_reset_tools_and_cascade.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust Mobile)
-- Description: Testing & Reset RPC for Admin & Super Admin:
--              1. reset_building_data(p_building_id UUID, p_mode TEXT)
--                 - 'residents_only': Wipes all apartments, obligations, payments,
--                   expenses, emergency claims, issues, polls, notifications, and
--                   resident users for the building while preserving the admin
--                   user and the building record (resets fund_balance = 0).
--                 - 'full_delete': Cascade-deletes the entire building, all child
--                   records, and the admin user profile (and auth.users), breaking
--                   circular foreign keys and signing admin out to onboarding.
-- ============================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reset_building_data(
    p_building_id UUID,
    p_mode TEXT
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, auth
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_caller_role TEXT;
    v_building RECORD;
    v_admin_id UUID;
    v_resident_user_ids UUID[];
    v_all_user_ids UUID[];
BEGIN
    -- 1. Security Check: Authenticated Caller
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', false,
            'error', 'يجب تسجيل الدخول أولاً لتنفيذ هذه العملية'
        );
    END IF;

    -- 2. Verify Building Exists
    SELECT * INTO v_building FROM public.buildings WHERE id = p_building_id;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', false,
            'error', 'العقار المحدد غير موجود في قاعدة البيانات'
        );
    END IF;

    -- 3. Verify Authorization (Super Admin OR Building Admin)
    SELECT role INTO v_caller_role FROM public.users WHERE id = v_caller_id;
    IF v_caller_role <> 'super_admin' AND (v_building.admin_id IS NULL OR v_building.admin_id <> v_caller_id) THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', false,
            'error', 'غير مصرح لك بإعادة تهيئة أو حذف بيانات هذه العمارة (مخصص لرئيس الاتحاد أو مدير النظام)'
        );
    END IF;

    v_admin_id := v_building.admin_id;

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Mode 1: 'residents_only' (تصفير العمارة مع بقاء حساب رئيس الاتحاد)
    -- ========================================================================
    IF p_mode = 'residents_only' THEN
        -- 1. Community Hub upvotes & votes
        DELETE FROM public.issue_upvotes WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.poll_votes WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 2. Community Hub issues & polls
        DELETE FROM public.building_issues WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.polls WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 3. Notifications
        DELETE FROM public.notifications WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 4. Emergency claims & claim shares
        DELETE FROM public.emergency_claim_shares WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.emergency_claims WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 5. Payments
        DELETE FROM public.payments WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 6. Expenses
        DELETE FROM public.expenses WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 7. App subscription invoices
        DELETE FROM public.app_subscription_invoices WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 8. Apartment obligations & arrears
        DELETE FROM public.apartment_obligations WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 9. Disconnect users from apartments before removing apartments
        UPDATE public.users
        SET apartment_id = NULL,
            apartment_number = NULL
        WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 10. Delete all apartments
        DELETE FROM public.apartments WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 11. Gather non-admin residents
        SELECT COALESCE(ARRAY_AGG(id), ARRAY[]::UUID[])
        INTO v_resident_user_ids
        FROM public.users
        WHERE building_id = p_building_id
          AND (v_admin_id IS NULL OR id <> v_admin_id);

        -- Delete non-admin residents from public.users
        IF array_length(v_resident_user_ids, 1) > 0 THEN
            DELETE FROM public.users WHERE id = ANY(v_resident_user_ids);

            -- Attempt to delete from auth.users so their emails/phones are freed up
            BEGIN
                DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id = ANY(v_resident_user_ids);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                -- If auth delete has external restrictions, public.users is already cleaned
                NULL;
            END;
        END IF;

        -- 12. Reset building fund balance to 0 and clear registration metadata
        UPDATE public.buildings
        SET fund_balance = 0.00,
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = p_building_id;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'mode', 'residents_only',
            'message', 'تم مسح جميع بيانات الشقق، المديونيات، السكان، والمصروفات بنجاح. العمارة الآن فارغة وجاهزة لإعادة التجربة مع بقاء حساب رئيس الاتحاد.'
        );

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Mode 2: 'full_delete' (حذف العمارة بالكامل وحساب رئيس الاتحاد)
    -- ========================================================================
    ELSIF p_mode = 'full_delete' THEN
        -- 1. Community Hub upvotes & votes
        DELETE FROM public.issue_upvotes WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.poll_votes WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 2. Community Hub issues & polls
        DELETE FROM public.building_issues WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.polls WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 3. Notifications
        DELETE FROM public.notifications WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 4. Emergency claims & claim shares
        DELETE FROM public.emergency_claim_shares WHERE building_id = p_building_id;
        DELETE FROM public.emergency_claims WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 5. Payments
        DELETE FROM public.payments WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 6. Expenses
        DELETE FROM public.expenses WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 7. App subscription invoices
        DELETE FROM public.app_subscription_invoices WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 8. Apartment obligations & arrears
        DELETE FROM public.apartment_obligations WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 9. Disconnect users from apartments
        UPDATE public.users
        SET apartment_id = NULL,
            apartment_number = NULL
        WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 10. Delete all apartments
        DELETE FROM public.apartments WHERE building_id = p_building_id;

        -- 11. Break circular foreign key between buildings.admin_id and users.id
        UPDATE public.buildings SET admin_id = NULL WHERE id = p_building_id;

        -- 12. Gather all user IDs of this building (including admin)
        SELECT COALESCE(ARRAY_AGG(id), ARRAY[]::UUID[])
        INTO v_all_user_ids
        FROM public.users
        WHERE building_id = p_building_id
           OR (v_admin_id IS NOT NULL AND id = v_admin_id);

        -- 13. Delete the building itself
        DELETE FROM public.buildings WHERE id = p_building_id;

        -- 14. Delete the users from public.users
        IF array_length(v_all_user_ids, 1) > 0 THEN
            DELETE FROM public.users WHERE id = ANY(v_all_user_ids);

            -- Attempt to delete from auth.users to release email/phone logins
            BEGIN
                DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id = ANY(v_all_user_ids);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                NULL;
            END;
        END IF;

        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', true,
            'mode', 'full_delete',
            'message', 'تم حذف العمارة بالكامل وجميع السجلات وحساب رئيس الاتحاد بنجاح. يمكنك الآن البدء من جديد كأنك تسجل لأول مرة.'
        );

    ELSE
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', false,
            'error', 'نوع العملية غير مدعوم. اختر إما residents_only أو full_delete'
        );
    END IF;
END;
$$;

-- Grant execution to authenticated users (internal checks enforce admin/super_admin role)
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.reset_building_data(UUID, TEXT) TO authenticated;

COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.reset_building_data IS 'دالة آمنة لإعادة تهيئة بيانات العمارة أو حذفها بالكامل مع حساب المدير لتسهيل وتسريع عمليات الاختبار والتطوير';


-- ===== FILE: D:\WorkSpace\اتحاد ملاك\supabase\migrations\015_setup_superadmin_and_dev_reset.sql =====

-- ============================================================================
-- Migration: 015_setup_superadmin_and_dev_reset.sql
-- Project: مدير اتحاد الملاك (Ittihad Trust)
-- Description: 
--   1. Fix protect_user_sensitive_columns trigger to permit 'admin' role creation
--      during building onboarding, and allow SQL Editor / internal RPC bypass.
--   2. Update create_building_for_admin with internal bypass flag.
--   3. Provision superadmin user in public.users for Supabase Auth account.
--   4. Update reset_building_data to accept p_secret_key bypass for one-click dev resets.
--   5. Grant execution permissions to authenticated and anon for dev resets.
-- ============================================================================

-- 1. Fix protect_user_sensitive_columns trigger
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_user_sensitive_columns()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_caller_building UUID;
    v_bypass TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Allow direct maintenance from SQL Editor (where auth.uid() is NULL)
    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Allow execution inside trusted internal RPCs (like create_building_for_admin)
    v_bypass := current_setting('app.rpc_internal_bypass', true);
    IF v_bypass = 'true' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    SELECT role, building_id INTO v_caller_role, v_caller_building
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = auth.uid();

    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        -- Only super_admin role is strictly restricted from self-assignment
        IF NEW.role = 'super_admin' AND COALESCE(v_caller_role, 'resident'::public.user_role) <> 'super_admin' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Cannot self-assign privileged role (super_admin).';
        END IF;

        -- If regular resident registration, default to pending
        IF NEW.role = 'resident' AND NEW.status IS NULL THEN
            NEW.status := 'pending';
        END IF;

        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
        -- Prevent unauthorized promotion to super_admin
        IF NEW.role = 'super_admin' AND v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Modifying user role to super_admin is strictly prohibited.';
        END IF;

        IF v_caller_role IS DISTINCT FROM 'super_admin' THEN
            -- Regular resident cannot modify their assigned building directly
            IF NEW.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.building_id AND OLD.building_id IS NOT NULL AND OLD.role = 'resident' THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Security Violation: Cannot change assigned building_id.';
            END IF;
        END IF;

        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- 2. Update check_user_update_permissions
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_user_update_permissions()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_role public.user_role;
    v_bypass TEXT;
BEGIN
    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    v_bypass := current_setting('app.rpc_internal_bypass', true);
    IF v_bypass = 'true' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    v_caller_role := public.get_my_role();
    IF v_caller_role = 'super_admin' THEN
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF auth.uid() = OLD.id THEN
        IF (OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.role OR
            OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.status OR
            OLD.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.building_id OR
            OLD.apartment_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.apartment_id OR
            OLD.apartment_number IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.apartment_number OR
            OLD.ownership_share_percent IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.ownership_share_percent) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: You cannot modify role, status, building_id, or apartment assignments directly.';
        END IF;

        IF OLD.role = 'resident' THEN
            IF (OLD.email IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.email OR
                OLD.phone IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.phone OR
                OLD.floor_number IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.floor_number OR
                OLD.residency_type IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.residency_type) THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Residents can only update full_name and push_token directly.';
            END IF;
        END IF;

        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    IF v_caller_role = 'admin' AND public.get_my_status() = 'active' AND OLD.building_id = public.get_my_building_id() THEN
        IF (OLD.role IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.role OR
            OLD.building_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.building_id) THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Building admins cannot modify role or building_id of users directly.';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Forbidden: Unauthorized update on users table.';
END;
$$;

-- 3. Update create_building_for_admin to set internal bypass flag
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_building_for_admin(
    p_full_name TEXT,
    p_phone TEXT,
    p_building_name TEXT,
    p_address TEXT,
    p_total_apartments INTEGER DEFAULT 16,
    p_default_monthly_dues NUMERIC(12, 2) DEFAULT 300.00,
    p_admin_apartment_number TEXT DEFAULT '1',
    p_registration_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_caller_id UUID;
    v_caller_email TEXT;
    v_existing_building UUID;
    v_building_id UUID;
    v_building_code TEXT;
    v_apartment_id UUID;
    v_pro_features JSONB;
    v_share_percent NUMERIC(5, 2);
BEGIN
    -- Set bypass flag for internal operations within this transaction
    PERFORM set_config('app.rpc_internal_bypass', 'true', true);

    -- 1. Identify admin strictly via auth.uid()
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: Authentication required (auth.uid() is null).';
    END IF;

    v_caller_email := COALESCE(auth.jwt()->>'email', (SELECT email FROM public.users WHERE id = v_caller_id));

    -- 2. Verify caller is not already assigned as admin of any building
    SELECT building_id INTO v_existing_building
    FROM public.users
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF v_existing_building IS NOT NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User is already assigned to a building.';
    END IF;

    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.buildings WHERE admin_id = v_caller_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'User is already an administrator of a building.';
    END IF;

    IF p_total_apartments IS NULL OR p_total_apartments <= 0 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Total apartments must be greater than zero.';
    END IF;

    -- Generate unique building code
    LOOP
        v_building_code := 'BLD-' || floor(100000 + random() * 900000)::text;
        EXIT WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.buildings WHERE building_code = v_building_code);
    END LOOP;

    -- Calculate equal ownership share
    v_share_percent := ROUND((100.0 / p_total_apartments)::NUMERIC, 2);

    -- Fetch default pro features config
    SELECT default_features_config INTO v_pro_features
    FROM public.subscription_plans
    WHERE code = 'pro';

    IF v_pro_features IS NULL THEN
        v_pro_features := '{"receipt_upload": true, "emergency_claims": true, "export_reports": true, "voting": true, "maintenance_tickets": true, "documents_archive": true}'::jsonb;
    END IF;

    -- 3. Create the building with 60-day Pro trial
    INSERT INTO public.buildings (
        name,
        address,
        building_code,
        registration_number,
        default_monthly_dues,
        fund_balance,
        total_apartments,
        plan_code,
        subscription_status,
        subscription_ends_at,
